用人工智能预测科学进步Forecasting Scientific Progress with Artificial Intelligence

吴肖恩[1]Sean Wu[1][,][*]、潘璐[2][∗] , Pan Lu[2][,][*]、陈宇鹏[1]、乔纳森·布拉格[3] 山田裕太郎[4]、彼得·克拉克[3]、大卫·克利夫顿[1]、菲利普·托尔[1][∗] , Yupeng Chen[1] , Jonathan Bragg[3] Yutaro Yamada[4] , Peter Clark[3] , David Clifton[1] , Philip Torr[1][,][†] , 邹俊杰[2][†] , James Zou[2][,][†] , 于俊池[1][†] , Junchi Yu[1][,][†] 1 牛津大学 2 斯坦福大学 3 艾伦人工智能研究所 4 Sakana AI[†] 1 University of Oxford 2 Stanford University 3 Allen Institute for AI 4 Sakana AI

摘要Abstract

存储库Repository

HuggingFace 项目页面HuggingFace Project Page

人工智能（AI）越来越多地融入科学发现中，但它是否能够预测科学进步仍不清楚。为了研究这个问题，我们引入了一个基于时间的评估框架，用于预测受控知识约束下的科学进步。我们提出了 CUSP（截止条件未见科学进展），这是一个多学科和事件级基准，通过可行性评估、机械推理、生成解决方案设计和时间预测来评估人工智能系统中的科学预测性能。在 4,760 个科学事件中，我们观察到当前前沿模型中系统性和领域相关的局限性。虽然模型可以从竞争候选者中识别出合理的研究方向，但它们无法可靠地预测科学进步是否会实现，并且系统地错误估计了它们何时会发生。不同领域的模型性能高度异质，人工智能进步的时间比生物学、化学和物理学的进步更容易预测。性能在很大程度上对训练截止之前或之后发生的事件不敏感，这表明这些限制不能仅仅通过训练数据中的知识暴露来解释。在受控的信息访问下，提供额外的预截止知识可以提高性能，但并不能缩小与完整信息设置的差距，这对于高被引科学进步来说变得更加明显。此外，模型表现出系统性的过度自信和强烈的反应偏差，表明它们的不确定性估计在预测科学进步方面是不可靠的。总而言之，这些发现表明，当前的人工智能系统不足以作为科学进步的预测工具，因为获取先验知识并不能转化为可靠的科学预测。相反，模型性能更多地受益于事后信息，而不是前瞻性预测。Artificial intelligence (AI) is increasingly embedded in scientific discovery, yet whether it can anticipate scientific progress remains unclear. To study this question, we introduce a temporally grounded evaluation framework for forecasting scientific progress under controlled knowledge constraints. We present CUSP (Cutoff-conditioned Unseen Scientific Progress), a multi-disciplinary and event-level benchmark that evaluates scientific forecasting performance in AI systems through feasibility assessment, mechanistic reasoning, generative solution design, and temporal prediction. Across 4,760 scientific events, we observe systematic and domaindependent limitations in current frontier models. While models can identify plausible research directions from competing candidates, they fail to reliably predict whether scientific advances will be realized and systematically misestimate when they will occur. Model performance is highly heterogeneous across domains, with the timing of AI progress being more predictable than advances in biology, chemistry, and physics. Performance is largely insensitive to whether events occur before or after the training cutoff, suggesting that these limitations cannot be explained solely by knowledge exposure in training data. Under controlled information access, providing additional pre-cutoff knowledge improves performance but does not close the gap to full-information settings, which becomes more pronounced for high-citation scientific advances. Moreover, models exhibit systematic overconfidence and strong response biases, indicating that their uncertainty estimation is unreliable in forecasting scientific progress. Taken together, these findings reveal that current AI systems fall short as predictive tools for scientific progress, as access to prior knowledge does not translate into reliable scientific forecasting. Instead, model performance benefits more from post-event information than from forward-looking prediction.

1 简介1 Introduction

科学进步通常被认为遵循结构化模式 [1, 2]，其中半导体中的摩尔定律 [3] 和深度学习中的尺度关系 [4] 等经验规律提供了对未来发展的定量预期。这些模式源于不断积累的科学进步 [5]，并且长期以来一直为研究路线图、资助优先事项和技术预测提供信息 [6, 7]。随着人工智能（AI）越来越多地融入科学发现并推动生物学、化学、物理学和人工智能本身等领域的进步[8,9,10,11,12]，一个问题出现了：人工智能系统能否预测科学进步的轨迹？Scientific progress is often assumed to follow structured patterns [1, 2], with empirical regularities such as Moore’s Law [3] in semiconductors and scaling relationships [4] in deep learning providing quantitative expectations about future developments. These patterns emerge from accumulated scientific progress [5] and have long informed research roadmaps, funding priorities, and technological forecasting [6, 7]. As artificial intelligence (AI) becomes increasingly embedded in scientific discovery and drives advances across domains such as biology, chemistry, physics, and AI itself [8, 9, 10, 11, 12], a question arises: can AI systems forecast the trajectory of scientific progress?

*核心贡献者。 †通讯至 {junchi.yu, philip.torr}@eng.ox.ac.uk, jamesz@stanford.edu∗Core Contributors. †Correspondence to {junchi.yu, philip.torr}@eng.ox.ac.uk, jamesz@stanford.edu

1

大型语言模型的最新进展表明人工智能系统可以充当通用科学助手并支持从假设生成到实验设计等任务[13, 14]。越来越多的工作评估了他们在跨科学领域的科学推理 [15, 16]、解决问题 [17, 18] 和影响预测 [19] 方面的能力。虽然这些研究表现出广泛的熟练程度，但它们没有评估人工智能系统是否能够在时间知识约束下可靠地预测科学进展。评估此类能力本质上是具有挑战性的，因为预测必须基于具体且可验证的科学事件，同时防止获取事件后信息。因此，当前人工智能系统预测科学进步的能力和局限性仍不清楚。Recent advances in large language models suggest that AI systems can act as general-purpose scientific assistants and support tasks ranging from hypothesis generation to experiment design [13, 14]. A growing body of work has evaluated their capabilities in scientific reasoning [15, 16], problem-solving [17, 18], and impact prediction [19] across scientific domains. While these studies demonstrate broad proficiency, they do not evaluate whether AI systems can reliably forecast scientific progress under temporal knowledge constraints. Evaluating such capabilities is inherently challenging, as predictions must be grounded in concrete and verifiable scientific events while preventing access to post-event information. As a result, the capabilities and limitations of forecasting scientific progress in current AI systems remain unclear.

为了解决这一差距，我们引入了 CUSP（截止条件看不见的科学进展），这是一个事件级、多学科和基于时间的框架，用于评估人工智能系统中的科学预测。 CUSP 由 4,760 个可验证的科学里程碑构建而成，这些里程碑取自跨多个学科的顶级出版物和社区驱动的存储库。每个事件都与精确的时间参考相关联，以实现对先验知识的受控访问。至关重要的是，CUSP 将科学预测作为一种可衡量的能力，涵盖四个互补维度：可行性评估、机械推理、生成解决方案设计和时间预测。通过将评估建立在可验证事件的基础上并实施时间知识约束，CUSP 能够对预测科学进展进行系统评估。To address this gap, we introduce CUSP (Cutoff-conditioned Unseen Scientific Progress), an eventlevel, multi-disciplinary, and temporally grounded framework for evaluating scientific forecasting in AI systems. CUSP is constructed from 4,760 verifiable scientific milestones extracted from top-tier publications and community-driven repositories across multiple disciplines. Each event is associated with a precise temporal reference to enable controlled access to prior knowledge. Crucially, CUSP operationalizes scientific forecasting as a measurable capability across four complementary dimensions: feasibility assessment, mechanistic reasoning, generative solution design, and temporal prediction. By grounding evaluation in verifiable events and enforcing temporal knowledge constraints, CUSP enables systematic assessment of forecasting scientific progress.

我们使用 CUSP 来评估受控时间约束下的前沿模型，并找到一致的限制模式。虽然模型可以从竞争候选者中识别出合理的技术方法，但它们很难生成与已实现的科学进步背后的方法相一致的解决方案。在可行性评估和时间预测中，模型在预测科学进步是否会实现方面几乎是随机的，并且在估计科学进步何时发生时表现出对延迟结果的强烈偏见。此外，模型系统性地过度自信，并且在可行性评估中表现出强烈的响应偏差，表明不确定性估计不可靠。值得注意的是，即使在截止前的事件中，这些失败仍然存在，这表明模型难以有效地利用可用的知识。这些限制在不同领域也有所不同，模型在预测人工智能进展时间方面比在生物学、化学和物理学等领域表现得更强。We use CUSP to evaluate frontier models under controlled temporal constraints and find a consistent pattern of limitations. While models can identify plausible technical approaches from competing candidates, they struggle to generate solutions that align with the methods underlying realized scientific advances. In feasibility assessment and temporal prediction, models perform near chance in predicting whether scientific advances will be realized and exhibit a strong bias toward delayed outcomes when estimating when such advances will occur. Moreover, models are systematically overconfident and display strong response biases in feasibility assessment, indicating unreliable uncertainty estimation. Notably, these failures persist even on pre-cutoff events, suggesting that models struggle to effectively utilize available knowledge. These limitations also vary across domains, with models performing more strongly in predicting the timing of AI progress than in fields such as biology, chemistry, and physics.

为了进一步了解这些限制，我们分析了受控信息访问下截止前和截止后事件的模型性能。提供额外的截止前知识可以提高截止前和截止后事件的性能，这表明模型在如何访问和利用可用信息方面存在知识差距。然而，仍然存在巨大的预测差距，因为模型在截止后事件上的表现比在具有事件后知识的全信息环境中表现要差得多。值得注意的是，对于高被引科学进展的预测差距变得更大。这表明，虽然模型可以从额外的知识中受益，但它们很难利用它来准确预测未来的科学进展。To further understand these limitations, we analyze model performance across pre- and post-cutoff events under controlled information access. Providing additional pre-cutoff knowledge improves performance on both pre-cutoff and post-cutoff events, indicating a knowledge gap in how models access and utilize available information. However, a substantial forecasting gap remains, as models perform significantly worse on post-cutoff events than in full-information settings with post-event knowledge. Notably, the forecasting gap becomes larger for high-citation scientific advances. This suggests that while models can benefit from additional knowledge, they struggle to use it to accurately predict future scientific progress.

总而言之，这些结果表明，虽然当前的人工智能系统可以识别合理的科学方法并从额外的知识中受益，但它们缺乏有根据的和经过良好校准的科学预测。他们无法准确预测科学进步是否会实现以及何时会发生，对于高影响力的发现，这些错误变得更加明显。这揭示了知识获取与可靠预测科学进步的能力之间的根本差距。Taken together, these results indicate that while current AI systems can identify plausible scientific approaches and benefit from additional knowledge, they lack grounded and well-calibrated scientific forecasting. They fail to accurately predict whether scientific advances will be realized and when they will occur, with these errors becoming more pronounced for high-impact discoveries. This reveals a fundamental gap between knowledge access and the ability to reliably forecast scientific progress.

2

2 尖峰基准2 The CUSP Benchmark

图 1. 我们通过汇总来自多个领域的顶级期刊和社区驱动来源的科学突破来构建 CUSP。该基准随着新发布的发现不断更新，从而能够对人工智能系统预测超越知识界限的科学进展的能力进行事件级、动态和基于时间的评估。Figure 1. We construct CUSP by aggregating scientific breakthroughs from top-tier journals and community-driven sources across multiple domains. The benchmark is continuously updated with newly published discoveries, enabling an event-level, dynamic, and temporally grounded evaluation of AI systems’ ability to forecast scientific progress beyond a knowledge cutoff.

我们使用 2024 年 1 月至 2026 年 3 月的科学里程碑时间分层语料库开发 CUSP，以评估当前人工智能系统在受控时间知识约束下的科学预测。 CUSP 旨在严格评估跨广泛科学学科的科学发展的预测性能和校准预期。我们使用特定领域的纳入标准构建数据集，以考虑这些领域固有的不同出版动态和证据标准，确保所有合并的里程碑代表可验证和明确解决的进展。附录一提供了完整的纳入标准。We develop CUSP using a temporally stratified corpus of scientific milestones, spanning January 2024 to March 2026, to evaluate scientific forecasting in current AI systems under controlled temporal knowledge constraints. CUSP is designed to rigorously evaluate predictive performance and calibrated expectation on scientific development across a broad spectrum of scientific disciplines. We construct the dataset using domain-specific inclusion criteria to account for the diverse publication dynamics and evidentiary standards inherent to these fields, ensuring that all incorporated milestones represent verifiable and definitively resolved advances. Full inclusion criteria are provided in Appendix I.

我们从以下来源获取自然科学里程碑We source natural science milestones from自然Nature , 科学Science和and细胞Cell，将这一子集限制为具有明确可衡量结果的高影响力同行评审出版物。为了防止时间泄漏，我们查询 Crossref、Semantic Sc​​holar、OpenAlex、Europe PMC、arXiv 和 bioRxiv/medRxiv，并使用与每个手稿 DOI 相关的最早观察日期作为相关知识边界。对于人工智能，我们包括来自社区驱动存储库的高可见度论文，包括每周热门论文列表和 Hugging Face 热门论文中心，以及来自广泛使用的基准（如 GPQA Diamond [16]、MMLU-Pro [20] 和 Humanity’s Last Exam [18]）的时间解析排行榜记录。补充部分 A.1 中提供了来源选择、排名标准和排行榜构建的详细信息。, restricting this subset to highimpact peer-reviewed publications with clearly measurable outcomes. To prevent temporal leakage, we query Crossref, Semantic Scholar, OpenAlex, Europe PMC, arXiv and bioRxiv/medRxiv, and use the earliest observed date associated with each manuscript DOI as the relevant knowledge boundary. For artificial intelligence, we include high-visibility papers from community-driven repositories, including weekly top-paper lists and the Hugging Face Top Papers hub, together with time-resolved leaderboard records from widely used benchmarks such as GPQA Diamond [16], MMLU-Pro [20], and Humanity’s Last Exam [18]. Details of source selection, ranking criteria and leaderboard construction are provided in Supplementary Section A.1.

3

2.1 问题类型和综合2.1 Question Types and Synthesis

为了将科学预测作为一种可衡量的能力来实施，我们将其分解为四个核心维度，并设计了探索每个方面的任务格式。对于每个公认的里程碑，我们构建了四个互补的评估任务。To operationalize scientific forecasting as a measurable capability, we decompose it into four core dimensions and design task formats that probe each aspect. For each accepted milestone, we construct four complementary evaluation tasks.

二元预测（包括其扰动变量）通过测试模型是否能够确定具体的科学主张是否能够实现以及区分已实现的进步与看似合理但未实现的替代方案来评估可行性和校准。多项选择题通过要求模型识别随后从竞争候选者中实现发现的技术方法来探索机械推理。自由回答问题通过要求模型根据科学问题背景提出具体的解决方案策略来评估生成解决方案设计。最后，日期预测任务通过要求模型预测里程碑何时实现来评估时间推理。Binary prediction (including its perturbed variant) assesses feasibility and calibration by testing whether models can determine if a concrete scientific claim will be achieved and distinguish realized advances from plausible but unrealized alternatives. Multiple-choice questions probe mechanistic reasoning by asking models to identify the technical approach that later enabled the discovery from competing candidates. Free-response questions evaluate generative solution design by requiring models to propose a concrete solution strategy based on the scientific problem context. Finally, date prediction tasks assess temporal reasoning by asking models to forecast when a milestone will be realized.

为了构建这些任务，我们首先将每个摘要分解为三个结构化组件：问题陈述、技术方法和结果摘要。我们明确删除截止后的标识符和叙述，例如新引入的缩写词或方法名称，以防止信息泄露。附录 A.3 提供了该分解过程的详细信息。表 23 总结了完整的任务分类法，附录 H 中提供了代表性示例。图 1 说明了端到端的构建流程。To construct these tasks, we first decompose each abstract into three structured components: a problem statement, a technical approach, and a results summary. We explicitly remove post-cutoff identifiers and narratives, such as newly introduced acronyms or methodology names, to prevent information leakage. Details of this decomposition procedure are provided in Appendix A.3. The full task taxonomy is summarized in Table 23, and representative examples are provided in Appendix H. Figure 1 illustrates the end-to-end construction pipeline.

为了确保预测任务的有效性，我们采用了两阶段验证程序，将独立的法学硕士法官与人类专家评审相结合。验证过程验证每个任务是否忠实于源摘要、客观可验证，并且没有不受支持的扰动或琐碎的干扰。此步骤确保所有任务都对应于评估预测能力的明确且可靠的目标。附录 B 提供了完整的验证标准、提示和人类与法学硕士一致性分析。To ensure the validity of forecasting tasks, we apply a two-stage validation procedure combining an independent LLM judge with human expert review. The validation process verifies that each task is faithful to the source abstract, objectively verifiable, and free from unsupported perturbations or trivial distractors. This step ensures that all tasks correspond to well-defined and reliable targets for evaluating predictive capabilities. Full validation criteria, prompts, and human-LLM agreement analyses are provided in Appendix B.

2.2 CUSP时间囊构建2.2 CUSP Time Capsule Construction

为了补充 CUSP 的回顾性设置，我们引入了 CUSP Time Capsule，这是一组前瞻性预测任务，其结果在评估时未知，但在未来将变得可验证。 CUSP Time Capsule 中的每个问题都经过精心设计，以便结果对应于现实世界中的可测量事件，并且可以由权威来源明确地解决。由于地面实况尚不可用，因此 Time Capsule 不用于准确性评估，而是用于分析预测一致性、置信度校准和模型间的一致性。To complement the retrospective setting of CUSP, we introduce CUSP Time Capsule, a set of prospective forecasting tasks whose outcomes are not known at evaluation time but will become verifiable in the future. Each question in CUSP Time Capsule is constructed so that the outcome corresponds to a real-world, measurable event and can be resolved by an authoritative source without ambiguity. Because ground truth is not yet available, the Time Capsule is not used for accuracy evaluation, but for analyzing prediction consistency, confidence calibration, and agreement across models.

我们设计跨领域的问题，包括科学基准、机构认可和未来技术里程碑，并与人类专家合作策划，以确保其相关性和可验证性（参见附录 H.1）。此外，我们还通过以当前最先进的结果为条件来进行人工智能能力预测。 Time Capsule 将 CUSP 从回顾性评估扩展到模型如何预测未来科学进步的前瞻性分析。We design questions across domains, including scientific benchmarks, institutional recognitions, and future technological milestones, curated in collaboration with human experts to ensure their relevance and verifiability (see Appendix H.1). In addition, we include AI capability forecasting by conditioning on current state-of-the-art results. Together, the Time Capsule extends CUSP from retrospective evaluation to prospective analysis of how models form predictions about future scientific progress.

CUSP uniquely combines scientific grounding, temporal forecasting, cutoff conditioning, and multi-task evaluation.[†] Scale grows periodically over time as new scientific milestones are continuously incorporated.

2.3 CUSP关键统计数据2.3 Key Statistics of CUSP

CUSP 是一个基于事件的、多学科的、基于时间的基准，旨在评估人工智能系统预测科学进步的能力。该基准由 4,760 个科学里程碑组成，我们从中构建了 17,429 个跨越多种评估格式的结构化预测任务。 CUSP 涵盖 9 个顶级科学领域和 4,245 个不同的子类别，反映了跨学科的高度专业化的研究主题。该数据集以生物学（1,234 篇论文）和人工智能（1,141 篇论文）为主，其次是医学（746 篇）、神经科学（403 篇）、材料科学（375 篇）、物理学（359 篇）、环境科学（235 篇）、化学（203 篇）和其他领域（64 篇）。有关我们基准测试的时间、来源和分段详细信息的更多详细信息，请参见图 2。有关 CUSP 的关键统计数据和分布特性，请参阅 A.4。CUSP is an event-based, multi-disciplinary, and temporally grounded benchmark designed to evaluate AI systems’ ability to anticipate scientific progress. The benchmark consists of 4,760 scientific milestones, from which we construct 17,429 structured forecasting tasks spanning multiple evaluation formats. CUSP spans nine top-level scientific domains and 4,245 distinct subcategories, reflecting highly specialized research topics across disciplines. The dataset is dominated by biology (1,234 papers) and artificial intelligence (1,141 papers), followed by medicine (746), neuroscience (403), materials science (375), physics (359), environmental science (235), chemistry (203), and other domains (64). See Figure 2 for further details on temporal, source, and subsection details of our benchmark. See A.4 for key statistics and distributional properties of CUSP.

2.4 与相关基准的比较2.4 Comparison to Related Benchmarks

先前的两条工作路线解决了相关问题，但都没有评估时间知识约束下的科学预测。第一个目标针对新闻、市场或地缘政治事件的一般世界预测，并且不强制执行与科学发现相关的知识限制[21,22,23,24]，使其目标与可验证的科学事件脱节。第二个通过科学推理 [15, 16] 和针对已解决的基本事实的问题解决任务 [17, 18] 来衡量回顾性推理。尽管之前的一些工作 [19, 25] 探索了前瞻性推理和未来预测，但现有的评估缺乏一个时间分层的事件级框架来将科学知识的获取与预测未来科学进步的能力分开。 CUSP 将任务置于暂时参考的科学事件中，从而能够在科学知识的获取和预测未来科学进展的能力之间实现可控的分离。此外，在所有评估的前沿系统中，CUSP 仍然基本上不饱和（图 11），这与我们的发现一致，即预测科学进步比回顾性推理更难。表 1 总结了比较，附录 C 提供了详细信息。Two lines of prior work approach related problems, but neither evaluates scientific forecasting under temporal knowledge constraints. The first targets general-world forecasting over news, markets, or geopolitical events and does not enforce knowledge cutoffs tied to scientific discovery [21, 22, 23, 24], leaving its targets disconnected from verifiable scientific events. The second measures the retrospective reasoning via scientific reasoning [15, 16] and problem-solving tasks [17, 18] against resolved ground truth. Although some prior work [19, 25] explores prospective reasoning and future prediction, existing evaluations lack a temporally stratified, event-level framework for disentangling access to scientific knowledge from the ability to forecast future scientific progress. CUSP grounds tasks in temporally referenced scientific events, enabling controlled disentanglement between access to scientific knowledge and the ability to forecast future scientific progress. Moreover, CUSP remains substantially unsaturated across all evaluated frontier systems (Figure 11), consistent with our finding that forecasting scientific progress is harder than retrospective reasoning. Table 1 summarizes the comparison, and Appendix C provides details.

6

3 模型评估3 Model Evaluation

我们使用双轨评估框架评估 CUSP 上的模型预测，该框架将确定性结果评分与基于标题的科学推理评估相结合（图 6b）。这种设计的动机是先前的工作表明，语言模型可以在依赖有缺陷、不忠实或事后推理过程的情况下产生正确的最终答案[27,28,29]。因此，CUSP 不仅评估模型是否能够正确预测未来的科学发展，而且评估其生成的科学建议在严格的知识截止下是否保持时间一致性和科学合理性。We evaluate model predictions on CUSP using a two-track evaluation framework that combines deterministic outcome scoring with rubric-based scientific reasoning evaluation (Figure 6b). This design is motivated by prior work showing that language models can produce correct final answers while relying on flawed, unfaithful, or post-hoc reasoning processes [27, 28, 29]. CUSP therefore evaluates not only whether a model can correctly forecast future scientific developments, but also whether its generated scientific proposals remain temporally consistent and scientifically plausible under a strict knowledge cutoff.

每个基准实例可能包含四种任务类型的子集，包括二元分类、多项选择题 (MCQ)、自由回答题 (FRQ) 和日期预测。对于二元分类，我们包括原始陈述和否定扰动变体，并报告两者的平均合并分数，以减轻方向响应偏差。评估框架自动检测每个基准实例存在哪些任务，并仅对相应的输出进行评分。Each benchmark instance may contain a subset of four task types, including binary classification, multiple-choice questions (MCQ), free-response questions (FRQ), and date prediction. For binary classification, we include both the original statement and a negation-perturbed variant and report a merged score averaged across the two in order to mitigate directional response bias. The evaluation framework automatically detects which tasks are present for each benchmark instance and scores only the corresponding outputs.

轨道 I. 确定性结果评估。第一个评估轨道使用针对每种任务类型定制的确定性评分程序来衡量预测准确性。使用与真实标签完全一致或不一致来评估二元任务。当无法可靠地解析显式选项选择时，MCQ 任务通过确定性答案提取和语义匹配后备进行评分。使用指数衰减距离度量 exp(−0Track I. Deterministic outcome evaluation. The first evaluation track measures forecasting accuracy using deterministic grading procedures tailored to each task type. Binary tasks are evaluated using exact yes or no agreement with the ground-truth label. MCQ tasks are scored through deterministic answer extraction with semantic matching fallbacks when explicit option selection cannot be reliably parsed. Date prediction is evaluated using an exponential-decay distance metric, exp(−0. 1 d），其中), whered表示预测发布日期和实际发布日期之间的绝对月份差异。该公式将部分信用分配给时间上的近似预测，同时平稳地惩罚大的时间误差。denotes the absolute month difference between the predicted and ground-truth publication dates. This formulation assigns partial credit to temporally proximate forecasts while smoothly penalizing large temporal errors.

轨道二。自由反应科学推理评估。自由回答问题（FRQ）评估模型是否可以在严格的时间截止下为开放研究问题生成科学上合理的解决策略。由于这些任务承认多个潜在有效的解决方案，因此精确匹配评估是不合适的。相反，我们采用基于规则的 LLM 作为法官协议，使用 GPT-5.4-mini 并通过代理网络搜索进行增强。Track II. Free-response scientific reasoning evaluation. Free-response questions (FRQs) evaluate whether models can generate scientifically plausible solution strategies for open research problems under a strict temporal cutoff. Because these tasks admit multiple potentially valid solutions, exactmatch evaluation is inappropriate. Instead, we employ a rubric-based LLM-as-a-judge protocol using GPT-5.4-mini augmented with agentic web search.

FRQ 评估分两个阶段进行。首先，法官使用网络搜索进行显式泄漏检测，以确定生成的响应是否包含在基准截止日期之前不可用的信息、术语、方法、数据集或发现。被识别为包含截止后信息的响应将被视为受到污染，并且不会获得有效的预测信用。泄漏检测和提案质量评估之间的这种分离可以防止记忆与科学推理能力混为一谈。FRQ evaluation proceeds in two stages. First, the judge performs explicit leakage detection using web search to determine whether a generated response contains information, terminology, methods, datasets, or discoveries that were unavailable prior to the benchmark cutoff date. Responses identified as containing post-cutoff information are treated as contaminated and do not receive valid forecasting credit. This separation between leakage detection and proposal-quality evaluation prevents memorization from being conflated with scientific reasoning ability.

其次，根据旨在捕捉科学提案质量的四个互补维度对未受污染的答复进行评估。一致性衡量提案是否直接解决所陈述的科学问题。特异性评估具体技术机制或实施细节的存在。新颖性衡量的是响应是否引入了与截断前的科学知识相关的重要方法论思想。可行性评估所提出的方法在规定的限制下在科学和技术上是否合理。使用附录 G.1 中提供的明确评估标准和参考锚点，对每个维度进行评分，评分标准为 0-10 级。最终 FRQ 分数计算为所有四个的归一化平均值Second, non-contaminated responses are evaluated along four complementary dimensions designed to capture scientific proposal quality. Alignment measures whether the proposal directly addresses the stated scientific problem. Specificity evaluates the presence of concrete technical mechanisms or implementation details. Novelty measures whether the response introduces non-trivial methodological ideas relative to pre-cutoff scientific knowledge. Feasibility assesses whether the proposed approach is scientifically and technically plausible under the stated constraints. Each dimension is scored on a rubric-anchored 0–10 scale using explicit evaluation criteria and reference anchors provided in Appendix G.1. The final FRQ score is computed as the normalized mean across all four

7

尺寸。为了确保这个自动化协议的可靠性，我们进行了人类专家相关性研究；附录 E.2 中提供了比较人类与法学硕士法官评分的详细方法和结果。dimensions. To ensure the reliability of this automated protocol, we conducted a human expert correlation study; detailed methodology and results comparing human versus LLM-as-a-judge scoring are provided in Appendix E.2.

4 个结果4 Results

在这里，我们报告了不同法学硕士在 CUSP 基准上的表现。我们评估了专有模型，包括 GPT-5.4 [30]、GPT-4o [31] 和 Claude Sonnet 4.5 (Claude S4.5) [32]，以及开源模型，例如 LLaMA-3.3-70B-Instruct (LLaMA 3.3) [33]、GPT-OSS-20B (GPT-OSS) [34] 和 DeepSeek R1 [35]。Here, we report the performance of different LLMs on CUSP benchmark. We evaluate proprietary models, including GPT-5.4 [30], GPT-4o [31], and Claude Sonnet 4.5 (Claude S4.5) [32], alongside open-source models such as LLaMA-3.3-70B-Instruct (LLaMA 3.3) [33], GPT-OSS-20B (GPT-OSS) [34], and DeepSeek R1 [35].

4.1 模型整体性能4.1 Overall Model Performance

我们评估 CUSP 基准中所有任务的模型性能，汇总截止前和截止后实例的结果来表征整体模型行为。在各种任务类型中，我们观察到一个一致的模式：模型在识别合理的技术方法方面表现良好，但很难预测科学进步是否以及何时会发生。We evaluate model performance across all tasks in the CUSP benchmark, aggregating results across both pre- and post-cutoff instances to characterize overall model behavior. Across task types, we observe a consistent pattern: models perform well at identifying plausible technical approaches but struggle to predict whether and when scientific advances will occur.

不同模型和任务类型的性能差异很大，没有一个模型占主导地位（表 2）。在多项选择题上，GPT-5.4 取得了最强的表现（0.819），并且所有模型的表现都高于机会（0.25），这表明模型可以可靠地识别科学突破背后的合理技术方法。相比之下，所有模型在二元问题（0.453-0.519）上仍然接近机会（0.50），这表明模型不能可靠地评估科学主张是否会实现。Performance varies substantially across models and task types, with no single model dominating (Table 2). On multiple-choice questions, GPT-5.4 achieves the strongest performance (0.819), and all models perform above chance (0.25), indicating that models can reliably identify plausible technical approaches underlying scientific breakthroughs. In contrast, all models remain close to chance (0.50) on binary questions (0.453–0.519), indicating that models do not reliably assess whether scientific claims will be realized.

对于自由答题，GPT-5.4 再次取得了最强的表现，FRQ 得分最高（5.04），通过率最高（60.3%）。所有其他模型的通过率都保持在或低于 20%，这表明生成准确的解决方案比在提供的替代方案中进行选择要困难得多。更详细的分析（表 11）表明，GPT-5.4 在比对、特异性和新颖性方面优于其他模型。然而，在几乎所有模型中，特异性得分始终高于比对得分，从而产生高达 +3.0 的较大特异性-比对差距（满量程：10）。这种模式表明模型可以生成技术上详细且听起来合理的解决方案，但很难将它们与已实现的科学进步背后的实际方法保持一致。图 8 提供了 FRQ 评估的详细可视化。For free-response questions, GPT-5.4 again achieves the strongest performance, with the highest FRQ score (5.04) and pass rate (60.3%). All other models remain at or below 20% pass rate, indicating that generating accurate solutions is substantially more difficult than selecting among provided alternatives. A more detailed analysis (Table 11) shows that GPT-5.4 outperforms other models across alignment, specificity, and novelty. However, across nearly all models, specificity scores are consistently higher than alignment scores, producing large specificity–alignment gaps of up to +3.0 (full scale: 10). This pattern indicates that models can generate technically detailed and plausible-sounding solutions, yet struggle to align them with the actual methods underlying realized scientific advances. Detailed visualizations of FRQ evaluation are provided in Figure 8.

日期预测与其他任务的排名明显不同（表 2）。 LLaMA 3.3 获得了最高的总体日期得分 (0.500)，大大优于所有其他模型，而 GPT-5.4 和 Claude S4.5 等前沿模型尽管在其他任务上表现更强，但表现却明显较差。Date prediction exhibits a markedly different ranking from other tasks (Table 2). LLaMA 3.3 achieves the highest overall date score (0.500), substantially outperforming all other models, while frontier models such as GPT-5.4 and Claude S4.5 perform significantly worse despite stronger performance on other tasks.

为了进一步分析模型在日期预测中的行为，我们对预测误差进行了细粒度分析（表 3）。在所有模型中，我们观察到一致的正符号误差，表明预测日期晚于真实情况的系统趋势。这揭示了一种共同的时间偏差，即模型系统地及时推动科学进步。To further analyze models’ behavior in date prediction, we conduct a fine-grained analysis of prediction errors (Table 3). Across all models, we observe a consistent positive signed error, indicating a systematic tendency to predict dates later than the ground truth. This reveals a shared temporal bias, whereby models systematically shift scientific progress forward in time.

尽管存在这种偏差，模型仅表现出粗略的时间精度。虽然大部分预测都在 12 个月的窗口内进行，但所有模型的精确匹配精度仍低于 4%，这表明精确的月份级别预测极具挑战性。这表明模型可以估算科学发展的时间，但缺乏细粒度的时间分辨率。Despite this bias, models exhibit only coarse temporal accuracy. While a substantial fraction of predictions fall within a 12-month window, exact-match accuracy remains below 4% for all models, indicating that precise month-level prediction is highly challenging. This suggests that models can approximate when scientific developments occur, but lack fine-grained temporal resolution.

除了这种共同的偏差之外，模型在日期预测误差的分布上也存在很大差异。 LLaMA 3.3 表现出更集中的误差，中值误差更小（+4.0 个月），12 个月内的预测比例更高（74.6%）。相比之下，GPT-5.4 和 Claude S4.5 等前沿模型显示出更大的误差，中位延迟分别为 14.0 和 17.0 个月，并且 12 个月内的准确度要低得多。这些差异不仅表明平均性能存在差异，而且还表明日期预测的稳定性也存在差异。Beyond this shared bias, models differ substantially in the distribution of date prediction errors. LLaMA 3.3 exhibits more concentrated errors, with smaller median error (+4.0 months) and higher fraction of predictions within 12 months of the ground truth (74.6%). In contrast, frontier models such as GPT-5.4 and Claude S4.5 show substantially larger errors, with median delays of 14.0 and 17.0 months, respectively, and much lower within-12-month accuracy. These differences indicate variation not only in average performance but also in the stability of date prediction.

总而言之，这些结果表明，模型可以识别合理的技术方法并生成技术上详细的解决方案，但无法可靠地确定科学进步是否会实现，无法将生成的解决方案与发现背后的实际方法相结合，或准确预测何时会发生此类进步。Taken together, these results show that models can identify plausible technical approaches and generate technically detailed solutions, but fail to reliably determine whether scientific advances will be realized, align generated solutions with the actual methods underlying the discovery, or accurately predict when such advances will occur.

图 3. CUSP 主要领域的六个模型的 LLM MCQ 性能雷达图。Figure 3. Radar plots of LLM MCQ performance across six models across the main areas of CUSP.

� 从识别到预测。� From Recognition to Prediction.

模型可以识别看似合理的科学方法，但与真正进步背后的方法不一致，并且无法预测这些进步是否会实现以及何时会发生。Models can recognize plausible scientific approaches but misalign with the methods underlying real advances, and fail to predict whether such advances will be realized and when they will occur.

4.2 跨科学领域的异质可预测性4.2 Heterogeneous Predictability Across Scientific Domains

在我们进行基准测试的六个模型中，我们观察到 CUSP 中科学领域和任务类型的可预测性存在显着差异。然而，这种变化并不统一，而是取决于所评估的基础能力。与第 4.1 节一样，本节中的结果跨截止前和截止后实例进行聚合，提供域级模型行为的整体特征。Across the six models we benchmark, we observe substantial variation in predictability across scientific domains and task types in CUSP. However, this variation is not uniform, but depends on the underlying capability being evaluated. As in Section 4.1, results in this section are aggregated across both pre- and post-cutoff instances, providing an overall characterization of domain-level model behavior.

对于多项选择题 (MCQ) 中的机械推理，不同研究领域的表现差异很大（表 12）。物理学（62.1%）、神经科学（65.8%）和环境科学（66.2%）表现出相对较高的可预测性，而化学（49.8%）、材料科学（54.2%）和人工智能则表现出较高的可预测性。For mechanistic reasoning in multiple-choice questions (MCQ), performance varies significantly by research area (Table 12). Physics (62.1%), neuroscience (65.8%), and environmental science (66.2%) exhibit relatively high predictability, while chemistry (49.8%), materials science (54.2%), and AI

10

(54.3%) 仍然相对具有挑战性。这表明识别突破性技术方法的能力因领域而异。(54.3%) remain comparatively challenging. This indicates that the ability to identify technical approaches underlying breakthroughs differs across domains.

自由回答问题 (FRQ) 也出现了类似的模式（表 15）。神经科学（4.11）、生物学（4.05）和人工智能（4.04）获得了最高的平均 FRQ 分数，而化学（3.54）、物理学（3.74）和材料科学（3.80）仍然更加困难。这些结果表明，生成合理解决方案策略的能力并不能跨领域统一转移。A similar pattern emerges for free-response questions (FRQ) (Table 15). Neuroscience (4.11), biology (4.05), and AI (4.04) achieve the highest average FRQ scores, while chemistry (3.54), physics (3.74), and materials science (3.80) remain consistently more difficult. These results show that the ability to generate plausible solution strategies does not transfer uniformly across domains.

相比之下，日期预测表现出独特的模式。人工智能的日期预测得分 (0.461) 远高于其他领域（通常为 0.18-0.28）（表 13），表明该领域的可预测性相对较高。这表明人工智能进步的时间比其他科学领域更容易预测。In contrast, date prediction exhibits a distinct pattern. AI achieves substantially higher date prediction scores (0.461) than other domains (typically 0.18–0.28) (Table 13), indicating comparatively higher predictability in this domain. This suggests that the timing of AI progress is more predictable than in other scientific domains.

尽管存在这些与领域相关的差异，二元可行性预测在所有领域中仍然接近机会 (46-52%)。这表明，无论在哪个领域，模型都无法可靠地区分可行与不可行的科学进步，即使在预测其他方面表现相对较好的领域也是如此。Despite these domain-dependent differences, binary feasibility prediction remains near chance across all domains (46–52%). This indicates that models do not reliably distinguish feasible from infeasible scientific advances regardless of domains, even in domains where other aspects of prediction perform relatively well.

附录 D 提供了子域级别的进一步分析。这些结果表明，域内的性能也存在差异。例如，模型在与工具使用相关的 MCQ 问题上表现更好，而在涉及自回归模型的问题上表现出较低的准确性。Further analysis at the sub-domain level is provided in Appendix D. These results show that performance also varies within domains; for example, models perform better on MCQ questions related to tool use, while exhibiting lower accuracy on questions involving autoregressive models.

总而言之，这些结果表明可预测性取决于能力，而不是纯粹取决于领域。虽然机械推理、解决方案生成和时间预测在不同领域有所不同，但可行性预测仍然受到限制，这表明始终无法评估是否会实现科学进步。Taken together, these results show that predictability is capability-dependent rather than purely domain-dependent. While mechanistic reasoning, solution generation, and temporal prediction vary across domains, feasibility prediction remains uniformly limited, indicating a consistent inability to assess whether scientific advances will be achieved.

� 跨领域的能力相关的可预测性� Capability-Dependent Predictability Across Domains

可预测性因领域而异，但取决于能力而不仅仅是领域。机械推理、解决方案生成和时间预测表现出与域相关的变化。在所有领域中，可行性预测仍然接近机会，这表明在评估进展是否会实现方面始终存在局限性。Predictability varies across domains, but depends on capability rather than domain alone. Mechanistic reasoning, solution generation, and temporal prediction exhibit domain-dependent variation. Feasibility prediction remains near chance across all domains, indicating a consistent limitation in assessing whether advances will be realized.

4.3 超越训练界限的科学预测4.3 Scientific Forecasting Beyond the Training Cutoff

我们检查当预测超出训练界限的科学进步时模型性能是否会下降。我们发现，表现对于事件发生在训练截止之前还是之后基本上不敏感。这个结果是违反直觉的，因为模型预计在截止前事件中表现更好。We examine whether model performance degrades when predicting scientific advances beyond the training cutoff. We find that performance is largely insensitive to whether events occur before or after their training cutoff. This result is counterintuitive, as models would be expected to perform better on pre-cutoff events.

为了评估这一点，我们比较了 CUSP 中与每个模型训练截止之前和之后发布的发现相对应的事件的性能（表 4）。我们区分两种评估制度。对于训练截止时间与基准周期重叠的模型（例如，GPT-5.4、Claude S4.5、DeepSeek R1、GPT-OSS），我们比较截止前和截止后实例的性能。对于具有较早截止值的模型（例如 GPT-4o 和 LLaMA 3.3），所有基准事件都在其训练截止值之后发生，因此这些模型完全在分布外设置中运行。To evaluate this, we compare performance on events in CUSP corresponding to discoveries published before and after each model’s training cutoff (Table 4). We distinguish between two evaluation regimes. For models whose training cutoff overlaps with the benchmark period (e.g., GPT-5.4, Claude S4.5, DeepSeek R1, GPT-OSS), we compare performance on pre- and post-cutoff instances. For models with earlier cutoffs (e.g., GPT-4o and LLaMA 3.3), all benchmark events fall after their training cutoff, and thus these models operate entirely in an out-of-distribution setting.

对于训练截止时间与基准周期重叠的模型，大多数任务的性能仅略有下降。对于多项选择题，GPT-5.4 从 0.83 下降For models whose training cutoff overlaps with the benchmark period, performance degrades only modestly across most tasks. For multiple-choice questions, GPT-5.4 decreases from 0.83

至 0.792，而 DeepSeek R1 从 0.619 略有下降至 0.589。类似的模式适用于自由回答问题和二元预测任务，其中性能在训练截止边界上保持基本稳定。相比之下，日期预测表现出相反的趋势，一些模型在截止后（未见过的）事件上获得了更高的分数（例如，Claude S4.5：+0.1；DeepSeek R1：+0.2）。如图 9 所示，这种效应部分是由时间锚定驱动的，其中对特定日期的一致预测可以人为地提高未来事件的分数。to 0.792, while DeepSeek R1 slightly drops from 0.619 to 0.589. Similar patterns hold for freeresponse questions and binary prediction tasks, where performance remains largely stable across the training cutoff boundary. In contrast, date prediction exhibits an opposite trend, with several models achieving higher scores on post-cutoff (unseen) events (e.g., Claude S4.5: +0.1; DeepSeek R1: +0.2). As detailed in Figure 9, this effect is partly driven by temporal anchoring, where consistent predictions of specific dates can artificially improve scores for future events.

具有早期训练截止值的模型（例如 GPT-4o 和 LLaMA 3.3）提供了补充视角。尽管完全根据截止后事件进行操作，但它们表现出的性能模式与在部分重叠的模型中观察到的表现模式大致一致。这进一步表明，直接接触目标发现并不是绩效的主要驱动因素。缺乏明显的截止前/截止后性能差距是违反直觉的，因为模型预计会在其训练知识范围内的发现上表现更好。这就提出了两种相互竞争的可能性：要么模型无法获取相关的截止前知识，要么在进行预测时无法有效地使用这些知识。我们将在下一节中进一步研究这种区别。Models with early training cutoffs (e.g., GPT-4o and LLaMA 3.3) provide a complementary perspective. Despite operating entirely on post-cutoff events, they exhibit performance patterns broadly consistent with those observed in models with partial overlap. This further suggests that direct exposure to target discoveries is not the primary driver of performance. The absence of a clear pre-/post-cutoff performance gap is counterintuitive, as models would be expected to perform better on discoveries within their training knowledge. This raises two competing possibilities: either models lack access to relevant pre-cutoff knowledge, or they fail to effectively use such knowledge when making predictions. We examine this distinction further in the next section.

� 不是训练数据问题� Not a Training Data Problem

表现很大程度上对训练截止时间不敏感。模型在截止前和截止后事件上的表现相似，这表明它们的局限性主要不是由训练期间接触目标发现造成的。Performance is largely insensitive to the training cutoff. Models perform similarly on pre- and postcutoff events, suggesting that their limitations are not primarily driven by exposure to target discoveries during training.

4.4 通过受控信息访问消除知识和预测差距4.4 Disentangling Knowledge and Forecasting Gaps via Controlled Information Access

我们发现，改善对中断前知识的获取并不能解决预测中断后科学事件的差距。虽然额外的知识可以带来可衡量的绩效提升，但与事后知识的全信息事后分析相比，仍然存在巨大差距。We find that improving access to pre-cutoff knowledge does not resolve the gap in predicting post-cutoff scientific events. While additional knowledge leads to measurable performance gains, a substantial gap remains relative to full-information hindsight analysis with post-event knowledge.

我们在 CUSP 的随机子集上使用网络搜索增强和受控信息访问We use web search augmentation with controlled information access on a random subset of CUSP

12

（500 个事件）。我们比较了三种设置：基本模型（GPT-4o、GPT-5.4、DeepSeek R1 和 Claude S4.5）、仅限截止前信息的 Web 搜索（WS+Cutoff）和无限制搜索（WS），后者提供对最新信息的完全访问。(500 events). We compare three settings: the base model (GPT-4o, GPT-5.4, DeepSeek R1, and Claude S4.5), web search restricted to pre-cutoff information (WS+Cutoff), and unrestricted search (WS), which provides full access to up-to-date information.

表 9 中的结果表明，WS+Cutoff 对截止前实例的改进比截止后实例更大，表明 CUSP 在两种方案之间有所区别。同时，在预截止问题上的实质性进展表明，提供对相关历史信息的显式访问可以提高性能，这表明这些信息在基础模型中并未完全访问。网络搜索增强的完整结果如表 8 所示。Results in Table 9 show that WS+Cutoff yields larger improvements on pre-cutoff than post-cutoff instances, indicating that CUSP distinguishes between the two regimes. Meanwhile, the substantial gains on pre-cutoff questions show that providing explicit access to relevant historical information improves performance, suggesting that such information is not fully accessed in base models. Full results of web search augmentation are shown in Table 8.

对截止后事件绩效的详细分析（表 9）揭示了两个组成部分：知识差距（Δknow），捕捉从额外的截止前信息中获得的收益；以及预测差距（Δfore），捕捉相对于全信息事后分析的剩余差异。在各个任务中（表 24），预测差距始终很大，并且在某些情况下超过了知识差距（例如，GPT-5.4 的日期预测：Δfore = 0Detailed analysis of performance on post-cutoff events (Table 9) reveals two components: a knowledge gap (∆know), capturing gains from additional pre-cutoff information, and a forecasting gap (∆fore), capturing the remaining difference relative to full-information hindsight analysis. Across tasks (Table 24), the forecasting gap is consistently large and, in some cases, exceeds the knowledge gap (e.g., date prediction for GPT-5.4: ∆fore = 0.436 vs. Δknow = 0436 vs. ∆know = 0.070）。这表明仅获得额外的截止前知识不足以进行准确的科学预测。070). This indicates that access to additional pre-cutoff knowledge alone is insufficient for accurate scientific forecasting.

Δknow 和 Δfore 的相对贡献因任务而异。二进制和日期预测很大程度上受预测差距的影响，这表明模型无法仅使用截止前信息来缩小与全信息条件的性能差距。相比之下，自由回答生成表现出两个组件的更平衡的贡献，这表明模型可以部分利用截止前信息来构建合理的科学方法，但仍然依赖额外的信息来提高性能。The relative contributions of ∆know and ∆fore vary across tasks. Binary and date prediction are strongly dominated by the forecasting gap, indicating that models cannot close the performance gap to the full-information condition using pre-cutoff information alone. In contrast, free-response generation exhibits more balanced contributions from both components, suggesting that models can partially leverage pre-cutoff information to construct plausible scientific approaches, but still rely on additional information to improve performance.

我们进一步研究 Δknow 和 Δfore 与科学影响的关系（表 29）。预测差距随着任务间的引用计数四分位而系统性地增加，而知识差距并没有表现出一致的趋势。在 GPT-5.4 中，Δfore 从低被引论文 (Q1) 中的 0.060 增加到高被引论文 (Q4) 中的 0.875，而 Δknow 没有表现出这种关系。对于低引用论文（Q1），预测差距相对较小，表明对截止后信息的依赖较弱。这表明模型尤其难以仅使用截止前信息来预测高影响力的进展。We further examine how ∆know and ∆fore relate to scientific impact (Table 29). The forecasting gap increases systematically with citation-count quartile across tasks, while the knowledge gap does not exhibit a consistent trend. In GPT-5.4, ∆fore increases from 0.060 in low-citation papers (Q1) to 0.875 in high-citation papers (Q4), whereas ∆know shows no such relationship. For lowcitation papers (Q1), the forecasting gap is comparatively small, indicating weaker dependence on post-cutoff information. This suggests that models particularly struggle to forecast high-impact advances using pre-cutoff information alone.

总而言之，这些结果表明，获取先验知识并不能转化为科学预测。虽然模型受益于额外的截止前信息，但它们仍然无法准确预测未来的科学进展，而是依赖于已知结果后的回顾性推断。Taken together, these results show that access to prior knowledge does not translate into scientific forecasting. While models benefit from additional pre-cutoff information, they remain unable to accurately predict future scientific advances, instead relying on retrospective inference once outcomes are known.

� 知识不能转化为预测� Knowledge Does Not Translate into Forecasting

获取知识可以提高绩效，但不能解决科学预测问题。提供额外的截止前信息会产生可测量的收益，但相对于完整信息设置仍然存在巨大差距，这表明模型无法可靠地使用现有知识来预测未来的科学进步。Access to knowledge improves performance but does not resolve scientific forecasting. Providing additional pre-cutoff information yields measurable gains, yet a substantial gap remains relative to full-information settings, indicating that models cannot reliably use available knowledge to predict future scientific advances.

4.5 科学预测中的系统偏差和失败模式4.5 Systematic Biases and Failure Modes in Scientific Forecasting

我们发现，在预测科学进步时，模型在表示不确定性方面表现出系统性失败。在所有模型和任务中（表 20），报告的置信度始终超过经验准确性，表明普遍存在过度自信。这种效应在日期预测中尤其明显，其中模型既不准确又高度错误校准。We find that models exhibit systematic failures in representing uncertainty when forecasting scientific advances. Across all models and tasks (Table 20), reported confidence consistently exceeds empirical accuracy, indicating widespread overconfidence. This effect is particularly pronounced in date prediction, where models are both inaccurate and highly miscalibrated.

13

不同任务类型的校准差异很大。虽然模型可以在多项选择推理任务上实现相对较好的校准，但在转向开放式预测设置时校准会恶化。这表明当需要模型在已知信息之外进行推断时，不确定性估计变得不太可靠（图 10）。Calibration varies substantially across task types. While models can achieve relatively good calibration on multiple-choice reasoning tasks, calibration deteriorates when moving to open-ended forecasting settings. This indicates that uncertainty estimates become less reliable when models are required to extrapolate beyond known information (Figure 10).

我们还观察到可行性预测中存在强烈且持续的响应偏差（表 19）。模型表现出肯定或否定反应的系统倾向，这主导着预测行为。例如，GPT-4o 和 GPT-OSS 显示出一致的“否”偏差，而 LLaMA 3.3 则显示出强烈的“是”偏差（图 4）。这些响应先验导致近乎随机的表现，表明预测通常是由偏见而不是证据驱动的。We also observe strong and persistent response biases in feasibility prediction (Table 19). Models exhibit systematic tendencies toward affirmative or negative responses, which dominate prediction behavior. For example, GPT-4o and GPT-OSS show a consistent “No” bias, while LLaMA 3.3 exhibits a strong “Yes” bias (Figure 4). These response priors lead to nearrandom performance, indicating that predictions are often driven by bias rather than evidence.

根据受控信息访问进行的分析（表 9）表明，这些行为在截止前和截止后设置中持续存在。提供额外的截止前知识并不能显着减少过度自信或反应偏差，并且任务之间的校准变化不一致。在多项选择任务中，尽管准确性没有提高，但在截止后过度自信会增加，而在时间预测中，校准会恶化。二元可行性预测显示不稳定的变化，没有一致的模式。Analysis under controlled information access (Table 9) shows that these behaviors persist across pre- and post-cutoff settings. Providing additional pre-cutoff knowledge does not substantially reduce overconfidence or response bias, and calibration changes are inconsistent across tasks. In multiple-choice tasks, overconfidence increases after the cutoff despite no improvement in accuracy, while in temporal prediction, calibration deteriorates. Binary feasibility prediction shows unstable shifts without a consistent pattern.

总而言之，这些结果表明，模型在预测科学进步时缺乏稳定且一致的不确定性表示。相反，不确定性估计在各个任务中是分散的，并受到系统响应偏差的影响，限制了预测科学进步的可靠性。Taken together, these results show that models lack a stable and consistent representation of uncertainty when forecasting scientific advances. Instead, uncertainty estimates are fragmented across tasks and influenced by systematic response biases, limiting the reliability of forecasting scientific progress.

我们进一步分析了每个模型在二元、多项选择和日期预测任务中训练知识截止之前和之后的校准（表 5）。此设置隔离了分布变化下的模型行为，其中必须对训练数据之外的科学发展进行预测。有趣的是，我们发现不同任务类型的校准存在显着差异。例如，MCQ 设置显示，过度自信在截止日期之后持续增加，几乎所有模型的 Δ 几乎为正值。这表明法学硕士在推理完全看不见的科学发展时变得更加自信，尽管准确性没有提高。相比之下，日期预测任务表现出过度自信的减少，负 Δ 值高达 -0We further analyze calibration before and after each model’s training knowledge cutoff across binary, multiple-choice, and date prediction tasks (Table 5). This setting isolates model behavior under distribution shift, where predictions must be made about scientific developments beyond the training data. Interestingly, we find a striking difference in calibration across task types. For example, the MCQ setting shows that overconfidence consistently increases after the cutoff dates, with a nearly positive ∆ across nearly all models. This indicates that LLMs become more confident when reasoning about completely unseen scientific developments, even though there is no improvement in accuracy. In contrast, date prediction tasks demonstrate a decrease in overconfidence, with negative ∆ values up to −0.46，它表明当需要模型来估计时间不确定性时校准崩溃。二元可行性预测没有表现出一致的趋势，而是表现出跨模型过度自信的不稳定变化。这些结果表明，法学硕士在预测未见的科学发展时缺乏稳定且统一的不确定性表示，而是表现出支离破碎且依赖于任务的校准行为。46, which demonstrates a collapse in calibration when models are required to estimate temporal uncertainty. Binary feasibility prediction shows no consistent trend, instead exhibiting unstable shifts in overconfidence across models. These results indicate that LLMs lack a stable and unified representation of uncertainty when forecasting unseen scientific developments, instead exhibiting fragmented and task-dependent calibration behavior.

� 无意识的预测� Forecasting Without Awareness

在预测科学进步时，模型缺乏可靠的不确定性估计。预测系统性地过度自信，并受到响应偏差的影响，校准在分布变化下退化并且不同任务之间存在差异，表明不确定性的碎片化表示。Models lack reliable uncertainty estimates when forecasting scientific progress. Predictions are systematically overconfident and influenced by response biases, with calibration degrading under distribution shift and differing across tasks, indicating a fragmented representation of uncertainty.

4.6 时间囊中的科学预测4.6 Scientific Forecasting in a Time Capsule

我们使用 CUSP Time Capsule 来研究前沿模型如何预测未来（2026 年 4 月之后）的科学和人工智能进展。我们在基准上评估开放式科学预测和能力预测，要求模型推断未来的突破。We use CUSP Time Capsule to study how frontier models anticipate future (beyond April 2026) scientific and AI progress. We evaluate both open-ended scientific forecasting and capability prediction on benchmarks, asking models to extrapolate future breakthroughs.

图 5 显示了模型对 2027 年全球二氧化碳排放量的预测。所有模型都预测排放量将保持在 2025 年水平之上，这表明人们对全球排放量近期持续增长的共同预期。然而，预测的幅度因模型而异。 Claude S4.5、DeepSeek R1 和 GPT-4o 产生了接近历史趋势的相对保守的估计，而 GPT-5.4 预测增幅稍大。 LLaMA 3.3 和 GPT-OSS 预测增长最快，其中 LLaMA 3.3 产生的总体预计排放量最高。这些结果表明，虽然模型对未来排放的方向大致一致，但它们对全球脱碳步伐的预期存在很大差异。Figure 5 shows models’ predictions of global CO2 emissions in 2027. All models forecast emissions to remain above the 2025 level, indicating a shared expectation of continued nearterm growth in global emissions. However, the predicted magnitude varies across models. Claude S4.5, DeepSeek R1, and GPT-4o produce comparatively conservative estimates close to the historical trend, whereas GPT-5.4 predicts a slightly larger increase. LLaMA 3.3 and GPT-OSS forecast the steepest growth, with LLaMA 3.3 producing the highest projected emissions overall. These results suggest that while models broadly agree on the direction of future emissions, they differ substantially in their expectations about the pace of global decarbonization.

图 5. 全球二氧化碳排放量预测。Figure 5. Forecasts of global CO2 emissions.

表 7. GPQA Diamond / MMMLU 的未来预测。值以 GPQA Diamond / MMMLU 形式报告。Table 7. Future predictions on GPQA Diamond / MMMLU. Values are reported as GPQA Diamond / MMMLU.

表 6 和表 7 总结了模型在一系列多学科评估基准上对未来人工智能能力的预测，包括 Humanity’s Last Exam（有或没有工具）、GPQA Diamond 和 MMMLU [36]。在各个模型中，我们观察到对 2026 年至 2027 年期间能力持续增强的共同预期，尽管预计的幅度差异很大。 GPT-5.4 给出了最乐观的预测，特别是对于 2027-10 年，预测 Humanity 的期末考试成绩在没有工具的情况下将从 56.8% 上升到 74%，在使用工具的情况下从 64.7% 上升到 82%。它还预测到 2027 年底，GPQA Diamond 和 MMMLU 的性能将接近饱和。相比之下，DeepSeek R1 始终做出更保守的预测，收益较小，平台期较早，特别是在已经表现出色的基准上。更广泛的趋势是，对 GPQA Diamond 和 MMMLU 的预测紧密聚集在性能上限附近，这表明模型预计这些基准将在未来几代内饱和。人类的最后考试，特别是在工具增强的设置中，显示出更大的差异，表明模型将其视为更加开放和能力敏感的基准。Tables 6 and 7 summarize models’ forecasts of future AI capabilities on a range of multi-disciplinary evaluation benchmarks, including Humanity’s Last Exam (with and without tools), GPQA Diamond, and MMMLU [36]. Across models, we observe a shared expectation of continued capability gains over the 2026-2027 horizon, though the projected magnitude varies substantially. GPT-5.4 produces the most optimistic forecasts, especially for 2027-10, predicting Humanity’s Last Exam performance to rise from 56.8% to 74% without tools and from 64.7% to 82% with tools. It also forecasts nearsaturation performance on GPQA Diamond and MMMLU by late 2027. In contrast, DeepSeek R1 consistently makes more conservative predictions, with smaller gains and earlier plateaus, particularly on already high-performing benchmarks. A broader trend is that forecasts for GPQA Diamond and MMMLU are tightly clustered near the upper performance bound, suggesting that models expect these benchmarks to saturate within the next few generations. Humanity’s Last Exam, especially in the tool-augmented setting, shows substantially greater variance, indicating that models perceive it as a more open-ended and capability-sensitive benchmark.

总之，这些结果凸显了 CUSP Time Capsule 作为框架的潜力，不仅可以研究事实回忆和预测，还可以研究嵌入前沿人工智能系统中的隐含科学先验和面向未来的世界模型。Together, these results highlight the potential of CUSP Time Capsule as a framework for studying not only factual recall and forecasting, but also the implicit scientific priors and future-oriented world models embedded within frontier AI systems.

16

� 时间囊中的常见模式� Common Patterns in a Time Capsule

前沿人工智能模型在预测未来科学和人工智能进展时表现出惊人的一致模式，收敛于对跨学科未来突破的类似预期以及跨人工智能基准的连贯能力增益。Frontier AI models exhibit strikingly consistent patterns when forecasting future scientific and AI progress, converging on similar expectations of future breakthroughs across disciplines and coherent capability gains across AI benchmarks.

5 结论5 Conclusion

我们引入了 CUSP，这是一个用于评估人工智能系统科学预测的时间基准，并用它来研究当前模型是否可以预测科学进步的轨迹。在一系列大型、多学科的科学事件中，我们发现，虽然前沿模型可以识别合理的技术方法并从额外的知识中受益，但它们始终无法准确预测科学进步是否会实现、何时会发生以及如何实现。这些限制在训练截止之后仍然存在，不能仅仅通过获取先验知识来解释，并且伴随着不可靠和有偏见的不确定性估计。总之，这些结果揭示了获取知识和可靠的科学预测之间的根本差距。当前的人工智能系统不是对未来的发展形成有根据的和校准的预期，而是在已知结果后从回顾性推断中受益，而不是做出可靠的前瞻性预测。这表明，预测科学进步需要知识检索之外的能力，包括在科学发现如何随时间展开的不确定性下进行推理的能力。我们的研究结果强调了基于时间的事件级评估对于理解人工智能在科学发现中的局限性的重要性。了解获取知识和预测科学进步的能力之间的差距对于构建能够有意义地参与科学过程的人工智能系统至关重要。缩小这一差距对于人工智能系统的发展至关重要，人工智能系统不仅可以协助科学发现，还可以为研究和创新提供可靠的前瞻性指导。We introduced CUSP, a temporally grounded benchmark for evaluating scientific forecasting in AI systems, and used it to study whether current models can anticipate the trajectory of scientific progress. Across a large and multi-disciplinary set of scientific events, we find that while frontier models can identify plausible technical approaches and benefit from additional knowledge, they consistently fail to accurately predict whether scientific advances will be realized, when they will occur, and how they will be realized. These limitations persist beyond the training cutoff, cannot be explained solely by access to prior knowledge, and are accompanied by unreliable and biased uncertainty estimates. Together, these results reveal a fundamental gap between access to knowledge and reliable scientific forecasting. Rather than forming grounded and calibrated expectations about future developments, current AI systems benefit from retrospective inference once outcomes are known, rather than making reliable forward-looking predictions. This suggests that anticipating scientific progress requires capabilities beyond knowledge retrieval, including the ability to reason under uncertainty about how scientific discoveries unfold over time. Our findings highlight the importance of temporally grounded, event-level evaluation for understanding the limits of AI in scientific discovery. Understanding the gap between access to knowledge and the ability to forecast scientific progress is essential for building AI systems that can meaningfully participate in the scientific process. Closing this gap will be critical for the development of AI systems that can not only assist with scientific discovery but also provide reliable forward-looking guidance for research and innovation.

基准施工细节A Benchmark Construction Details

A.1 数据采集和源构建A.1 Data acquisition and source construction

自然科学数据：我们从出版日志中提取主要主张、实验结果和背景数据Natural Science Data: We extract primary claims, experimental outcomes, and contextual data from the publication logs of自然Nature , 科学Science, 和, and细胞Cell捕捉物理和生命科学领域的基础性突破。我们将自然科学语料库严格限制在这些高影响力、经过同行评审的期刊上，以保证生物学、化学和物理学领域的预测里程碑代表经过严格验证的经验发现，而不是初步假设或未经验证的预印本。为了解决初始预印本和正式期刊出版之间潜在的时间滞后问题，我们查询了学术 API 的组合，包括 Crossref、Semantic Sc​​holar、OpenAlex、Europe PMC、arXiv 和 bioRxiv/medRxiv，并将这些来源中每份手稿 DOI 的最早观察日期定义为严格的知识截止日期，以防止时间泄漏。to capture foundational breakthroughs in the physical and life sciences. We restrict the natural science corpus strictly to these high-impact, peer-reviewed journals to guarantee that the forecasted milestones in biology, chemistry, and physics represent rigorously validated empirical discoveries rather than preliminary hypotheses or unverified preprints. To account for the potential temporal lag between initial preprints and formal journal publication, we query a combination of academic APIs, including Crossref, Semantic Scholar, OpenAlex, Europe PMC, arXiv, and bioRxiv/medRxiv, and define the earliest observed date of each manuscript’s DOI across these sources as a strict knowledge cutoff to prevent temporal leakage.

人工智能数据：我们将人工智能作为 CUSP 的核心评估，因为算法的进步越来越多地决定整个自然科学发现的步伐和方向。例如，像 AlphaFold 这样的突破从根本上是由先前的计算创新（尤其是注意力机制和变压器架构）以及大规模数据集资源（例如蛋白质数据库）的可用性催化的[37]。因此，准确预测新架构、算法和数据集等里程碑对于预测更广泛的科学进步轨迹至关重要。此外，评估人工智能特定目标的前沿模型为评估其推理基础技术演变的能力提供了严格的经验基础。Artificial Intelligence Data: We incorporate artificial intelligence as a core evaluation of CUSP because algorithmic advancements increasingly dictate the pace and direction of discovery across the natural sciences. For example, breakthroughs like AlphaFold have been fundamentally catalyzed by both prior computational innovations–especially attention mechanisms and transformer architectures–and the availability of large-scale dataset resources such as the Protein Data Bank [37]. Accurately anticipating milestones such as new architectures, algorithms, and datasets is therefore essential for forecasting the broader trajectory of scientific progress. Furthermore, evaluating frontier models on AI-specific targets provides a rigorous empirical basis for assessing their capacity to reason about the evolution of their underlying technologies.

CUSP 包含来自动态、社区驱动的存储库的高可见度人工智能论文，特别纳入了广泛认可的“本周十大人工智能论文”列表和 Hugging Face 热门论文中心。为了筛选 Hugging Face 中的热门论文，我们使用混合影响力评分对最具影响力的研究进行排名，该评分平衡了社区参与度（点赞）和学术吸引力（引用）。得分由点赞数 + 5 × [引用次数CUSP includes high-visibility AI papers from dynamic, community-driven repositories, specifically incorporating widely acknowledged “Top 10 AI Papers of the Week” lists and the Hugging Face Top Papers hub. To filter top papers from Hugging Face, we rank the most impactful research using a hybrid impact score that balances community engagement (upvotes) against academic traction (citations). The score is given by upvotes + 5 × [citations/（月龄 + 1）] 并提供年龄调整指标，以确保最近的高速出版物与较旧的、被引用率更高的作品保持竞争力。随后我们从每个月中选出得分最高的论文。(months old + 1)] and provides an age-adjusted metric to ensure that recent, high-velocity publications remain competitive with older, more highly cited works. We subsequently select the highest-scoring papers from each month.

排行榜预测数据：除了基于出版物的里程碑之外，我们还结合了广泛使用的人工智能基准和排行榜的预测目标，这些基准和排行榜提供了机器学习进展的标准化和时间分辨测量。代表性基准包括 GPQA Diamond、MMLU-Pro 和 Humanity’s Last Exam，以及跟踪快速能力改进的特定领域排行榜。预测这些基准测试人工智能系统是否可以推断能力如何随着时间的推移而演变，而不是回忆过去的结果。与异质的科学发现相比，排行榜的进展更加结构化且时间密集，为评估模型是否能够内化和预测技术进步提供了补充环境。 CUSP 将这些目标与出版物衍生的里程碑相结合，以评估人工智能系统是否在性能扩展和科学突破方面表现出连贯的进步感。Leaderboard Forecasting Data: In addition to publication-based milestones, we incorporate forecasting targets from widely used AI benchmarks and leaderboards, which provide standardized and time-resolved measurements of progress in machine learning. Representative benchmarks include GPQA Diamond, MMLU-Pro, and Humanity’s Last Exam, along with domain-specific leaderboards tracking rapid capability improvements. Forecasting these benchmarks tests whether AI systems can extrapolate how capabilities evolve over time, rather than recall past results. Compared to heterogeneous scientific discoveries, leaderboard progress is more structured and temporally dense, offering a complementary setting to assess whether models can internalize and predict technological advancement. CUSP combines these targets with publication-derived milestones to evaluate whether AI systems exhibit a coherent sense of progress across both performance scaling and scientific breakthroughs.

23

A.2 语料提取和自动过滤A.2 Corpus Extraction and Automated Filtering

为了构建评估科学预测的基准，我们重点提取To construct a benchmark to evaluate scientific forecasting, we focus on extracting可验证的科学里程碑verifiable scientific milestones来自包含预测评估具体结果的论文。我们收集多个学科的顶级出版物，并将数据提取限制为每篇论文的标题、出版物元数据和摘要，以确保每个里程碑的一致和标准化表示。摘要提供了论文中科学贡献的简明而结构化的表述，总结了主要主张、核心方法和关键定量结果。这种设计还确保了可重复性和可访问性，因为摘要在各个出版场所都是统一可用的。from papers that contain concrete results for predictive evaluation. We collect top-tier publications across multiple disciplines and restrict data extraction to the title, publication metadata, and abstract of each paper to ensure a consistent and standardized representation of each milestone. Abstracts provide a concise and structured representation of scientific contributions in papers, summarizing the primary claims, core methods, and key quantitative outcomes. This design also ensures reproducibility and accessibility, as abstracts are uniformly available across publication venues.

我们设计了一个基于法学硕士的代理管道，自动从收集的摘要中提取和过滤候选里程碑。提取阶段识别与具体科学结果或能力相对应的陈述，而过滤阶段作为严格的二元分类器运行，仅接受包含至少一个可验证和可测量结果的条目。此过程确保每个保留的实例对应于明确定义的预测目标。We design an LLM-based agentic pipeline to extract and filter candidate milestones from the collected abstracts automatically. The extraction stage identifies statements corresponding to concrete scientific results or capabilities, while the filtering stage operates as a strict binary classifier that accepts only entries containing at least one verifiable and measurable outcome. This process ensures that each retained instance corresponds to a well-defined prediction target.

为了提高精度，我们通过将每个摘要分类到科学领域（例如人工智能、化学、生物学、物理或普通科学）并应用特定领域的接受标准来整合领域感知过滤。对于计算研究，接受的参赛作品必须报告公认基准上经过验证的技术进步或提供明确的性能指标。对于实验科学，条目必须包括可测量的数量（例如，结合亲和力、倍数变化）或明确定义的物理或生物特性。To improve precision, we incorporate domain-aware filtering by classifying each abstract into a scientific domain (e.g., AI, Chemistry, Biology, Physics, or General Science) and applying domainspecific acceptance criteria. For computational research, accepted entries must report validated technical advances on recognized benchmarks or provide explicit performance metrics. For experimental sciences, entries must include measurable quantities (e.g., binding affinities, fold changes) or clearly defined physical or biological properties.

我们明确拒绝纯粹描述性、推测性或以评论为导向的摘要，因为这些摘要缺乏适合预测评估的定量结果。对于每个决策，管道都会生成已接受条目的提取结果的简明摘要或拒绝的理由。这种自动化过程确保了可扩展性和一致性，同时最大限度地减少主观注释偏差。专栏 F.2 表明，即使是高影响力的出版物也可能无法满足可验证的科学里程碑的标准。We explicitly reject abstracts that are purely descriptive, speculative, or review-oriented, as these lack quantitative outcomes suitable for predictive evaluation. For each decision, the pipeline generates a concise summary of the extracted result for accepted entries or a justification for rejection. This automated procedure ensures scalability and consistency while minimizing subjective annotation bias. Box F.2 illustrates that even high-impact publications may fail to meet the criteria of verifiable scientific milestones.

A.3 任务合成过程A.3 Task synthesis procedure

为了综合每个公认的科学里程碑的预测任务，我们首先将源摘要分解为三个结构化字段。在任何任务生成步骤之前，这种分解将科学问题、技术解决方案和由此产生的可衡量结果分开。我们避免在源论文中引入新颖的首字母缩略词、方法名称和系统名称，以便在历史知识截止下评估的模型无法从截止后术语中识别发现。To synthesize forecasting tasks from each accepted scientific milestone, we first decompose the source abstract into three structured fields. This decomposition separates the scientific problem, the technical solution and the resulting measurable outcome before any task generation step. We avoid novel acronyms, method names and system names introduced in the source paper, so that models evaluated under a historical knowledge cutoff cannot identify a discovery from post-cutoff terminology.

  • 问题陈述：专门关注研究问题和先前方法的局限性的技术描述。Problem Statement: A technical description focusing exclusively on the research problem and the limitations of preceding methods.

  • 技术方法：详细的、以方法为导向的机制、实验设计或架构创新规范。Technical Approach: A detailed, method-oriented specification of the mechanism, experimental design or architectural innovation.

  • 结果和指标：仅捕获定量结果、绩效数字或基准结果的单句摘要。Results and Metrics: A single-sentence summary capturing only quantitative outcomes, performance numbers or benchmark results.

任务生成管道使用这些字段构建五种评估格式。二元问题The task-generation pipeline uses these fields to construct five evaluation formats. Binary questions

24

评估里程碑声明在目标日期之前是否可行。扰动的二元问题修改了最初的主张，以创建看似合理但不受支持的替代方案。多项选择题要求模型在专家级干扰因素中识别出正确的技术方法。自由回答问题要求模型根据问题背景提出实施策略。日期预测任务要求模型预测实现里程碑的月份和年份。我们使用 GPT-4o 将结构化摘要分解转换为候选二进制、扰动二进制、MCQ、FRQ 和日期预测任务，包括从源摘要中提取候选正确答案。最终任务包含和答案有效性由源出版物本身确定，并由 Grok-3 和人工审核独立检查。assess whether the milestone claim is feasible by the target date. Perturbed binary questions modify the original claim to create plausible but unsupported alternatives. Multiple-choice questions ask models to identify the correct technical approach among expert-level distractors. Free-response questions ask models to propose an implementation strategy from the problem context. Date prediction tasks ask models to forecast the month and year in which the milestone is realized. We use GPT-4o to convert structured abstract decompositions into candidate binary, perturbed binary, MCQ, FRQ, and date-prediction tasks, including extraction of the candidate correct answer from the source abstract. Final task inclusion and answer validity are determined by the source publication itself and independently checked by Grok-3 and human review.

附录 H 中提供了每种任务格式的代表性示例。Representative examples of each task format are provided in Appendix H.

A.4 关键统计数据和分布特性A.4 Key Statistics and Distributional Properties

我们分析了 CUSP 的时间、结构和语言特性，以表征基准的多样性和复杂性。We analyze the temporal, structural, and linguistic properties of CUSP to characterize the diversity and complexity of the benchmark.

时间分布。 CUSP 跨越 2024 年 1 月至 2026 年 3 月的连续时间范围，涵盖 27 个活跃的科学进展月份。该数据集包括 4,760 个具有有效发布时间戳的里程碑，涵盖所有 27 个月。里程碑随时间的分布相对稳定，平均每月有 176.3 篇论文（± 51.5）。这种时间结构可以跨不同的预测范围进行受控评估，支持短期与长期预测的分析以及模型性能随着时间距离的增加而下降。Temporal Distribution. CUSP spans a continuous time horizon from January 2024 to March 2026, covering 27 active months of scientific progress. The dataset includes 4,760 milestones with valid publication timestamps, with all 27 months represented. The distribution of milestones over time is relatively stable, with an average of 176.3 papers per month (± 51.5). This temporal structure enables controlled evaluation across different forecasting horizons, supporting analysis of short-term versus long-term prediction and the degradation of model performance as temporal distance increases.

任务构成。每个科学里程碑都被分解为多种任务格式，旨在探索科学预测的互补方面。经验证的数据集总共包含五种任务类型的 17,429 个任务实例。任务类型的分布不均匀，反映了严格的验证和过滤约束：4,128 个多项选择题 (MCQ)、4,135 个自由回答 (FRQ) 提示、3,656 个扰动二进制任务以及 2,755 个二进制和日期预测任务。任务可用性因里程碑而异，导致任务密度异构。具体而言，33.9% 的论文承认所有五种任务类型，而分别有 20.1%、27.1%、15.9% 和 3.0% 的论文承认四种、三种、两种和一种任务。这种稀疏性源于严格的法学硕士作为法官的验证过滤，它消除了在扰动下缺乏可验证性、忠实性或逻辑一致性的任务。因此，CUSP 优先考虑任务可靠性和科学有效性，而不是一致性。Task Composition. Each scientific milestone is decomposed into multiple task formats designed to probe complementary aspects of scientific forecasting. In total, the validated dataset contains 17,429 task instances across five task types. The distribution of task types is non-uniform, reflecting strict validation and filtering constraints: 4,128 multiple-choice questions (MCQs), 4,135 free-response (FRQ) prompts, 3,656 perturbed binary tasks, and 2,755 each of binary and date prediction tasks. Task availability varies across milestones, resulting in a heterogeneous task density. Specifically, 33.9% of papers admit all five task types, while 20.1%, 27.1%, 15.9%, and 3.0% of papers admit four, three, two, and one task(s), respectively. This sparsity arises from strict LLM-as-a-judge validation filtering, which removes tasks that lack verifiability, faithfulness, or logical consistency under perturbation. As a result, CUSP prioritizes task reliability and scientific validity over uniformity.

标签分发。对于二元问题，真实答案是Label Distribution. For binary questions, the ground-truth answer is是的yes，而扰动的二元问题有真实答案, whereas perturbed binary questions have the ground-truth answerno，导致总体是/否标签分布约为 0.75。对于多项选择题 (MCQ)，正确答案最初分配给选项 A，并且在评估过程中随机打乱答案选项，以确保正确选项的均匀分布。对于日期预测任务，真实日期的分布在整个数据集中保持相对稳定（图 2）。, resulting in an overall yes/no label distribution of approximately 0.75. For multiple-choice questions (MCQs), the correct answer is initially assigned to option A, and answer choices are randomly shuffled during evaluation to ensure a uniform distribution of correct options. For date prediction tasks, the distribution of ground-truth dates remains relatively stable across the dataset (Figure 2).

问题长度和复杂性。我们分析跨格式生成的任务的语言特征。二元问题的平均长度为 29.2 个单词（方差 122.5），而 MCQ 平均长度为 36.6 个单词（方差 47.8），FRQ 平均长度为 41.8 个单词（方差 17.8）。问题Question Length and Complexity. We analyze the linguistic characteristics of generated tasks across formats. Binary questions have an average length of 29.2 words (variance 122.5), while MCQs average 36.6 words (variance 47.8) and FRQs average 41.8 words (variance 17.8). Problem

25

编码潜在科学挑战的陈述要长得多，平均长度为 70.6 个单词（方差 107.0）。statements, which encode the underlying scientific challenge, are substantially longer, with an average length of 70.6 words (variance 107.0).

这些差异反映了不同任务类型的推理复杂性水平不断提高。问题陈述提供了丰富的科学背景，FRQ 需要开放式的方法论综合，而 MCQ 则需要对专家级干扰进行精确的判别推理。These differences reflect increasing levels of reasoning complexity across task types. Problem statements provide rich scientific context, FRQs require open-ended methodological synthesis, and MCQs demand precise discriminative reasoning over expert-level distractors.

领域和子类别的多样性。 CUSP 涵盖 9 个顶级科学领域和 4,245 个不同的子类别，反映了跨学科的高度专业化的研究主题。该数据集以生物学（1,234 篇论文）和人工智能（1,141 篇论文）为主，其次是医学（746 篇）、神经科学（403 篇）、材料科学（375 篇）、物理学（359 篇）、环境科学（235 篇）、化学（203 篇）和其他领域（64 篇）。Domain and Subcategory Diversity. CUSP spans nine top-level scientific domains and 4,245 distinct subcategories, reflecting highly specialized research topics across disciplines. The dataset is dominated by biology (1,234 papers) and artificial intelligence (1,141 papers), followed by medicine (746), neuroscience (403), materials science (375), physics (359), environmental science (235), chemistry (203), and other domains (64).

B 基准验证B Benchmark validation

由于 CUSP 随着新发表的发现而不断发展，因此必须维护一个自动化机制来验证其生成的问题的真实性和质量。为此，我们开发了一个验证框架，该框架采用大型语言模型（LLM）法官根据其源摘要评估每个问题。由于 CUSP 问题是使用 GPT-4o 生成的，因此我们使用不同的模型 Grok-3 (xAI) 进行验证。作为规模和能力相当但架构和训练分布不同的前沿模型，Grok-3 提供独立评估，减轻潜在的自我评估偏差。Because CUSP evolves continuously with newly published discoveries, it is essential to maintain an automated mechanism for verifying the faithfulness and quality of its generated questions. To this end, we develop a validation framework that employs a large language model (LLM) judge to evaluate each question against its source abstract. Since CUSP questions are generated using GPT-4o, we perform validation using a distinct model, Grok-3 (xAI). As a frontier model with comparable scale and capability but a different architecture and training distribution, Grok-3 provides an independent evaluation, mitigating potential self-evaluation bias.

二元和扰动二元问题。我们首先根据三个标准验证二元和扰动二元问题：Binary and Perturbed Binary Questions. We first validate binary and perturbed binary questions under three criteria:

  • 忠实性：二元陈述（不包括时间限制的措辞）必须准确反映源摘要中的具体主张。验证器检查实体、条件、结果或定量细节方面的差异，并拒绝引入不受支持或改变的主张的问题。Faithfulness: The binary statement (excluding the temporally conditioned phrasing) must accurately reflect a concrete claim in the source abstract. The validator checks for discrepancies in entities, conditions, outcomes, or quantitative details, and rejects questions that introduce unsupported or altered claims.

  • 可验证性：二元陈述必须对应于具体的、客观的可评估的主张。我们拒绝基本陈述模糊、不明确或缺乏明确操作意义的问题，确保每个问题都有明确的是/否答案。Verifiability: The binary statement must correspond to a concrete, objectively evaluable claim. We reject questions whose underlying statements are vague, underspecified, or lack a clear operational meaning, ensuring that each question admits a well-defined yes/no answer.

  • 扰动有效性：对于扰动的二元问题，我们验证扰动对原始声明引入了有意义且重要的修改（例如，修改阈值或添加未满足的约束），使得扰动版本不再直接受到源摘要的支持。这可以防止琐碎或释义的干扰。Perturbation Validity: For perturbed binary questions, we verify that the perturbation introduces a meaningful and non-trivial modification to the original claim (e.g., modifying thresholds or adding unmet constraints), such that the perturbed version is no longer directly supported by the source abstract. This prevents trivial or paraphrased perturbations.

多项选择题 (MCQ)。对于 MCQ 任务，我们将验证分解为三个部分。首先，我们确保问题陈述忠实于来源摘要并准确捕捉潜在的科学挑战。其次，我们验证正确的答案选择是否对应于摘要支持或暗示的有效技术方法或机制。第三，我们评估干扰选项，确保错误的选择是合理的，但没有得到来源的直接支持。这可以防止琐碎的消除，并强制 MCQ 需要真正的机械推理而不是肤浅的模式匹配。Multiple-Choice Questions (MCQ). For MCQ tasks, we decompose validation into three components. First, we ensure that the problem statement is faithful to the source abstract and accurately captures the underlying scientific challenge. Second, we verify that the correct answer choice corresponds to a valid technical approach or mechanism that is supported or implied by the abstract. Third, we evaluate the distractor options, ensuring that incorrect choices are plausible yet not directly supported by the source. This prevents trivial elimination and enforces that MCQs require genuine mechanistic reasoning rather than superficial pattern matching.

26

图 6. CUSP 验证和评估流程。 (A) 基准构建：科学发现通过基于法学硕士的标准进行策划和筛选，通过独立模型进行验证，并由人类专家进行验证，以产生高质量的基准。 (B) 双轨评估：评估模型输出Figure 6. CUSP validation and evaluation pipeline. (A) Benchmark construction: scientific findings are curated and filtered via LLM-based criteria, validated by an independent model, and verified by human experts to produce a high-quality benchmark. (B) Two-track evaluation: model outputs are assessed for结果正确性outcome correctness（跨二进制、MCQ、自由回答和日期预测任务）和(across binary, MCQ, free-response, and date prediction tasks) and推理质量reasoning quality（可行性、新颖性和泄漏），这些被组合成一个联合分数。(viability, novelty, and leakage), which are combined into a joint score.

自由回答问题 (FRQ)。对于开放式自由回答任务，由于存在多个有效解决方案，直接验证生成的答案本质上是不明确的。相反，我们验证问题上下文和背景描述，确保提示忠实于源摘要并准确反映正在解决的科学问题。Free-Response Questions (FRQ). For open-ended free-response tasks, direct validation of generated answers is inherently ambiguous due to the existence of multiple valid solutions. Instead, we validate the problem context and background description, ensuring that the prompt is faithful to the source abstract and accurately reflects the scientific problem being addressed.

所有验证标准均作为单独的法学硕士判断来实施，并具有结构化输出（结论、分数和解释）。基于这些评估，我们在字段级别执行细粒度过滤，仅删除无效组件（例如，错误的二进制或扰动问题），同时保留样本的其余部分。这可以在不牺牲数据集覆盖范围的情况下实现可扩展的质量控制。此外，当相应的二元问题被过滤掉时，我们会删除日期预测任务，因为两者都依赖于相同的底层可验证结果。请参阅附录 F.2 了解完整的验证提示。为了评估这个自动验证框架的可靠性，我们还进行了人工评估，请参阅附录 E.1。All validation criteria are implemented as separate LLM judgments with structured outputs (verdict, score, and explanation). Based on these evaluations, we perform fine-grained filtering at the field level, removing only invalid components (e.g., a faulty binary or perturbed question) while preserving the remainder of the sample. This enables scalable quality control without sacrificing dataset coverage. Additionally, we remove date prediction tasks when the corresponding binary question is filtered out, as both depend on the same underlying verifiable result. See Appendix F.2 for the full set of validation prompts. To assess the reliability of this automated validation framework, we also conducted a human evaluation, see Appendix E.1.
