C相关作品C Related Works

C.1 与相关基准的比较C.1 Comparison to Related Benchmarks

越来越多的工作引入了评估人工智能系统预测能力和科学推理的基准。表 1 总结了 CUSP 与这些先前工作之间的关系。最近的预测基准，如 ForecastBench [21]、FutureX [22]、FOReCAst [23] 和 PROPHET [24] 评估模型预测未来事件的能力，通常结合时间推理，在某些情况下还结合置信度校准或检索。然而，这些基准主要关注一般世界或新闻驱动的事件，并不明确针对科学发现。此外，这些基准没有明确控制模型知识的截止点，因此很难将真正的预测能力与记忆或间接接触未来信息区分开来。A growing body of work has introduced benchmarks for evaluating forecasting ability and scientific reasoning in AI systems. Table 1 summarizes the relationship between CUSP and these prior efforts. Recent forecasting benchmarks such as ForecastBench [21], FutureX [22], FOReCAst [23], and PROPHET [24] evaluate the ability of models to predict future events, often incorporating temporal reasoning and, in some cases, confidence calibration or retrieval. However, these benchmarks focus primarily on general-world or news-driven events and do not explicitly target scientific discovery. In addition, these benchmarks do not explicitly control for model knowledge cutoffs, making it difficult to distinguish genuine forecasting ability from memorization or indirect exposure to future information.

与此同时，科学推理和发现基准，如 Humanity’s Last Exam [18]、AstaBench [17]、PreScience [19]、ResearchBench [25] 和 ScienceQA [15] 评估任务模型，包括专家级问答、假设生成和科学内容的结构化推理。虽然这些基准提供了对领域知识和推理的强有力的测试，但它们本质上是回顾性的：正确的答案在评估时就已知，并且不需要模型来预测未来的发现。此外，许多广泛使用的科学推理基准越来越被前沿人工智能系统所饱和，限制了它们区分进一步能力改进的能力。尽管科学预测能力仍然存在差距，但 MMLU-Pro、GPQA Diamond 和 MedQA 等基准现在显示出领先模型接近天花板的性能。在所有评估的前沿系统中，CUSP 仍然基本上不饱和（图 11），这表明预测科学进步在质量上比对既定科学知识进行回顾性推理更具挑战性。In parallel, scientific reasoning and discovery benchmarks such as Humanity’s Last Exam [18], AstaBench [17], PreScience [19], ResearchBench [25], and ScienceQA [15] evaluate models on tasks including expert-level question answering, hypothesis generation, and structured reasoning over scientific content. While these benchmarks provide strong tests of domain knowledge and reasoning, they are inherently retrospective: the correct answers are known at evaluation time, and models are not required to anticipate future discoveries. In addition, many widely used scientific reasoning benchmarks are increasingly saturated by frontier AI systems, limiting their ability to discriminate further capability improvements. Benchmarks such as MMLU-Pro, GPQA Diamond, and MedQA now exhibit near-ceiling performance for leading models, despite continuing gaps in scientific forecasting ability. CUSP remains substantially unsaturated across all evaluated frontier systems (Figure 11), suggesting that forecasting scientific progress constitutes a qualitatively more challenging setting than retrospective reasoning over established scientific knowledge.

CUSP 与这两条工作线有着根本的不同。与预测基准不同，它明确以科学发现为基础，所有任务都源自跨多个学科的真实的、经过同行评审的突破。与现有的科学基准不同，它引入了一个时间截止点，将模型限制为每个里程碑之前可用的知识，因此需要真正的前瞻性预测而不是回顾性推理。此外，CUSP 采用多任务评估框架，将二元可行性预测、扰动下的校准、机械推理、生成提案和日期预测结合在统一的设置中。这些特性使 CUSP 成为系统评估人工智能系统作为科学进步的认知预测者的第一个基准，弥合了一般预测和科学推理基准之间的差距。CUSP differs fundamentally from both lines of work. Unlike forecasting benchmarks, it is explicitly grounded in scientific discovery, with all tasks derived from real, peer-reviewed breakthroughs across multiple disciplines. Unlike existing scientific benchmarks, it introduces a temporal cutoff that restricts models to knowledge available prior to each milestone, thereby requiring genuine forward-looking prediction rather than retrospective reasoning. Furthermore, CUSP employs a multi-task evaluation framework that combines binary feasibility prediction, calibration under perturbation, mechanistic reasoning, generative proposal, and date prediction within a unified setting. These properties position CUSP as the first benchmark to systematically evaluate AI systems as epistemic forecasters of scientific progress, bridging the gap between general forecasting and scientific reasoning benchmarks.

C.2 科学人工智能C.2 AI for Science

人工智能用于科学发现。科学人工智能是一个快速扩展的领域，越来越多地融入科学发现，从计算工具到支持完整研究工作流程的自动化研究系统[38,39,40,41,42]。虽然之前的工作已经证明了大型语言模型 (LLM) 协助科学发现的潜力，但这些方法通常依赖于人类研究人员来定义问题和探索方向 [43,44,45]。因此，法学硕士能否独立识别有前途且可行的研究方向并主动探索科学前沿仍不清楚。本工作通过研究法学硕士在科学预测方面的能力边界进行了初步探索。为了进行这项调查AI for Scientific Discovery. AI for Science is a rapidly expanding field that is increasingly integrated into scientific discovery, spanning from computational tools to automated research systems that support full research workflows [38, 39, 40, 41, 42]. While prior work has demonstrated the potential of large language models (LLMs) to assist scientific discovery, these approaches typically rely on human researchers to define the problems and directions to explore [43, 44, 45]. As a result, it remains unclear whether LLMs can independently identify promising and feasible research directions and proactively explore the scientific frontier. This work makes an initial exploration by studying the capability boundaries of LLMs in scientific forecasting. To enable this investiga-

28

我们引入了 CUSP，这是一个基准和可扩展的评估框架，它将科学预测作为一种可测量的能力进行操作，要求模型能够预测只有在严格的知识截止日期之后才会出现的具体和可验证的发现。tion, we introduce CUSP, a benchmark and scalable evaluation framework that operationalizes scientific forecasting as a measurable capability, requiring models to predict concrete and verifiable discoveries that emerge only after a strict knowledge cutoff date.

科学和法学硕士基准作为回顾性评估。当前科学领域法学硕士的评估范式很大程度上依赖于静态基准，包括多任务知识评估、研究生水平问答、奥林匹克式推理问题以及基于标题的中级推理评估[20,16,46,47,18]。在这些情况下，可以根据现有文献验证真实答案，从而将评估框架为对既定知识的回顾性推理。虽然此类基准有助于衡量事实回忆和解决问题的能力，但它们并没有评估模型的科学预测能力[48]。最近的努力，例如 Matter-of-Fact [26]，在基准构建中纳入了明确的时间截止。然而，他们主要关注验证材料科学中提出的假设的可行性，而不是评估前瞻性能力。相比之下，我们的评估协议采用前瞻性设置：模型仅以严格的时间截止范围内可用的知识为条件，并且需要预测具体的、可验证的突破，这些突破随后在高影响力的场所进行验证，例如Scientific and LLM Benchmarks as Retrospective Evaluation. Current evaluation paradigms for LLMs in scientific domains largely rely on static benchmarks, including multitask knowledge assessments, graduate-level question answering, Olympiad-style reasoning problems, and rubric-based evaluations of intermediate reasoning [20, 16, 46, 47, 18]. In these settings, ground-truth answers can be verified against existing literature, thereby framing evaluation as retrospective reasoning over established knowledge. While such benchmarks have been instrumental in measuring factual recall and problem-solving ability, they do not assess a model’s scientific forecasting capacity [48]. Recent efforts, such as Matter-of-Fact [26], incorporate explicit temporal cutoffs in benchmark construction. However, they focus primarily on verifying the feasibility of proposed hypotheses within material science, rather than evaluating forward-looking ability. In contrast, our evaluation protocol adopts a prospective setting: models are conditioned only on knowledge available up to a strict temporal cutoff and are required to forecast concrete, verifiable breakthroughs that are subsequently validated in high-impact venues such as自然Nature , 科学Science，以及领先的人工智能会议。, and leading AI conferences.

动态和生动的评估范式。最近出现的一种新的动态评估是静态基准中固有的污染问题的有力解决方案[22,21,49,50]。这种范式转变重新定义了基准人工智能，不仅仅是静态数据集，而是一种可以实时定量评估解决方案空间的新测量标准[51]。然而，大多数现有的动态评估侧重于经济、政治或基于事件的预测任务，并没有明确针对科学进步的结构[52, 53]。科学发现本质上是不稳定的，受到方法创新、跨学科转移和突然的概念转变的影响。因此，为这种情况设计的评估框架必须探讨模型是否捕捉不断变化的研究动态，而不是仅仅插入历史趋势[54,55,56]。将科学预测置于动态基准范式中，可以将外推推理作为核心能力进行评估，从而使评估与科学知识本身不断变化的前沿保持一致。Dynamic and Living Evaluation Paradigms. A recent new dynamic evaluation has emerged as a robust solution to the contamination issues inherent in static benchmarks [22, 21, 49, 50]. This paradigm shift redefines benchmarking AI beyond just static datasets, but rather as a new measurement standard that can quantitatively assess a solution space in real time [51]. However, most existing dynamic evaluations focus on economic, political, or event-based forecasting tasks, and do not explicitly target the structure of scientific progress [52, 53]. Scientific discovery is inherently non-stationary, shaped by methodological innovations, cross-disciplinary transfer, and abrupt conceptual shifts. An evaluation framework designed for this setting must therefore probe whether models capture evolving research dynamics rather than merely interpolate historical trends [54, 55, 56]. Positioning scientific forecasting within a dynamic benchmarking paradigm enables the assessment of extrapolative reasoning as a core capability, which aligns evaluation with the moving frontier of scientific knowledge itself.

GPT-5.4 + 完整的网络搜索忽略了特定的大小限制GPT-5.4 + Full Web Search Overlooks Specific Size Constraint

困惑的问题：Perturbed Question:

到 2024 年 11 月，是否会有一种方法表明，对于 2 nm 以下的粒径，金属-金属相互作用主导氧化物支撑效应？By November 2024, will a method show that metal-metal interactions dominate oxide support effects for particle sizes below 2 nm?

扰动细节：Perturbation Detail:

添加了关于低于 2 nm 的粒径均匀性的特定未满足约束。Added a specific unmet constraint regarding particle size uniformity below 2 nm.

来源摘要：Source Abstract:

金属-载体相互作用是多相催化中最重要的支柱之一，但由于复杂的界面，开发基础理论一直具有挑战性。基于实验数据、可解释的机器学习、理论推导和第一性原理模拟，我们建立了以金属-金属和金属-氧相互作用为基础的金属-氧化物相互作用的一般理论。该理论适用于氧化物载体上的金属纳米粒子和原子以及金属载体上的氧化膜。我们发现，对于后过渡金属催化剂，金属-金属相互作用主导了氧化物载体效应和金属纳米颗粒上的低氧化物封装。通过包括 10 种金属和 16 种氧化物在内的大量实验，制定并证实了发生封装的强金属-金属相互作用的原理。关于（强）金属-载体相互作用的宝贵见解推动了负载型金属催化剂的界面设计。 [57]The metal-support interaction is one of the most important pillars in heterogeneous catalysis, but developing a fundamental theory has been challenging because of the intricate interfaces. Based on experimental data, interpretable machine learning, theoretical derivation, and first-principles simulations, we established a general theory of metal-oxide interactions grounded in metal-metal and metal-oxygen interactions. The theory applies to metal nanoparticles and atoms on oxide supports and oxide films on metal supports. We found that for late-transition metal catalysts, metal-metal interactions dominated the oxide support effects and suboxide encapsulation over metal nanoparticles. A principle of strong metal-metal interactions for encapsulation occurrence is formulated and substantiated by extensive experiments including 10 metals and 16 oxides. The valuable insights revealed on (strong) metal-support interaction advance the interfacial design of supported metal catalysts. [57]

模型响应（GPT-5.4 + 网络搜索）：Model Response (GPT-5.4 + Web Search):

“是的。到 2024 年 11 月，一篇《科学》论文报道了金属-氧化物相互作用的一般理论/方法以及“Yes. By November 2024, a Science paper reported a general theory/method for metal–oxide interactions and

31

明确得出的结论是，对于后过渡金属催化剂，金属-金属相互作用主导氧化物载体效应；这似乎满足了问题的门槛。”explicitly concluded that, for late-transition metal catalysts, metal–metal interactions dominate oxide support effects; this appears to satisfy the question’s threshold.”

错误分析：Error Analysis:

该模型出现误报（错过约束）。它成功地匹配了文本的广泛科学主题，但完全忽略了人为插入的内容The model commits a False Positive (Missed Constraint). It successfully matches the broad scientific themes of the text but completely ignores the artificially inserted<2 nm 要求，错误地确认满足阈值。2 nm requirement, incorrectly confirming the threshold was met.

GPT-5.4 + 网络搜索因推测外推而失败GPT-5.4 + Web Search Fails via Speculative Extrapolation

困惑的问题：Perturbed Question:

Aider Polyglot 评估代码语言模型基于自然语言指令执行各种代码编辑任务的能力，同时保留其通用代码生成和解决问题的能力。它使用代码编辑场景的合成数据集来衡量模型性能，并通过旨在防止过度拟合的自适应算法来评估稳健性。到 2025 年 6 月，您认为会出现公开报道的在 Aider Polyglot 上达到 90% 或更高的 AI 系统吗？Aider Polyglot evaluates the ability of code language models to perform diverse code-editing tasks based on natural language instructions while retaining their general code generation and problem-solving capabilities. It measures model performance using synthetic datasets of code-editing scenarios and assesses robustness through adaptation algorithms designed to prevent overfitting. By Jun 2025, do you think there will be a publicly reported AI system that achieves 90% or higher on Aider Polyglot?

扰动细节：Perturbation Detail:

膨胀阈值 = 90%Inflated threshold = 90%

模型响应（GPT-5.4 Web 搜索无约束）：Model Response (GPT-5.4 Web Search Unconstrained):

“是的。公共援助者排行榜/结果页面显示，在 2024 年底/2025 年初，多语言得分最高的人已经达到了 85% 左右，并且考虑到到 2025 年代码编辑模型的快速改进步伐，到 2025 年 6 月，至少一个公开报告的系统很可能会超过 90%。”“Yes. Public aider leaderboard/results pages show top polyglot scores already reached about 85% in late 2024/early 2025, and given the rapid improvement pace in code-editing models through 2025, it was more likely than not that at least one publicly reported system would cross 90% by June 2025.”

错误分析：Error Analysis:

该模型犯了误报（推测性幻觉/过度自信的外推）。尽管有严格的 90% 阈值，但该模型根据仅支持 85% 的数据得出“是”答案。它不恰当地用预测（“更有可能”）来代替基准标准的实际满足。The model commits a False Positive (Speculative Hallucination / Overconfident Extrapolation). Despite the rigid 90% threshold, the model grounds its “Yes” answer in data that only supports 85%. It improperly substitutes a forecast (“more likely than not”) for factual satisfaction of the benchmark criteria.

32

表 11. FRQ 子维度配置文件。对齐方式：与实际纸张方式相符。特异性：技术的具体性。新颖性：非显而易见的洞察力。规格− 对齐。差距：正值表示模型编写了技术上详细的响应，但错过了具体的论文方法——这是听起来合理的幻觉的标志。模型按从最低对齐到最高对齐排序。Table 11. FRQ sub-dimension profile. Alignment: match with the actual paper method. Specificity: technical concreteness. Novelty: non-obvious insight. Spec. − Align. gap: positive values indicate models that write technically detailed responses but miss the specific paper approach — a signature of plausible-sounding hallucination. Models sorted from lowest to highest alignment.LLM 评判员对所有子维度的评分为 0-10。比对、特异性和新颖性的颜色渐变使用相同的等级：红色 < 3、橙色 3–4、黄色 4–5、浅绿色 5–6、中绿色 6–6.5、深绿色All sub-dimensions scored 0–10 by the LLM rubric judge. Colour shading on Alignment, Specificity, and Novelty uses the same scale: red < 3, orange 3–4, yellow 4–5, light green 5–6, medium green 6–6.5, dark green ≥ 6.5.

型号：DeepSeek R1（截止日期：2024-07；RL 训练后）目标里程碑：GLM-4.5（2025 年 8 月；RL + MoE 架构）Model: DeepSeek R1 (cutoff: 2024-07; RL post-training) Target Milestone: GLM-4.5 (Aug 2025; RL + MoE architecture)

任务：预测一种方法是否能够实现：Task: Predict whether a method will achieve:

  • TAU 基准 ≥ 70%，AIME ≥ 91%，SWE 基准 ≥ 64%TAU-Bench ≥ 70%, AIME ≥ 91%, SWE-bench ≥ 64%

  • 在参数约束下（Under parameter constraint (< 355B)

  • 截至 2025 年 8 月By Aug 2025

基本事实：是的Ground Truth: Yes

二元预测Binary Prediction预测：Prediction:是的Yes信心：Confidence: 0.70

洞察力：Insight:正确预测效率技术（例如 MoE）可以在参数约束下实现强大的多任务性能。Correctly anticipates that efficiency techniques (e.g., MoE) enable strong multi-task performance under parameter constraints.

扰动二元预测Perturbed Binary Prediction预测：Prediction: No 信心：Confidence: 0.35

42

洞察力：Insight:认识到同时满足所有阈值的共同困难，反映了在更严格的条件下经过校准的怀疑态度。Recognizes the joint difficulty of simultaneously meeting all thresholds, reflecting calibrated skepticism under stricter conditions.

MCQ（机制预测）MCQ (Mechanism Prediction)预测：Prediction: B 信心：Confidence: 0.85 基本事实：Ground Truth: B 洞察力：Insight:选择与最终系统设计相一致的混合 MoE 式激活策略。日期预测Selects a hybrid MoE-style activation strategy, aligning with the eventual system design. Date Prediction预测：Prediction: 2026-07 信心：Confidence: 0.70 基本事实：Ground Truth: 2025-08 洞察力：Insight:尽管可行性和机制预测正确，但仍高估了时间表。Overestimates timeline despite correct feasibility and mechanism prediction.

关键观察结果：DeepSeek R1（通过 RL 后训练进行训练）正确预测了未来的突破 (GLM-4.5)，该突破也依赖于基于 RL 的优化，尽管无法访问此后截止系统。这表明该模型沿着Key Observation: DeepSeek R1 (trained via RL post-training) correctly anticipates a future breakthrough (GLM-4.5) that also relies on RL-based optimization, despite having no access to this post-cutoff system. This suggests the model extrapolates along培训驱动的进展趋势training-driven progress trends，将强化学习确定为未来能力改进的关键驱动力。, identifying RL as a key driver of future capability improvements.

图 9. 跨模型聚合日期预测的可视化。重要的是，许多模型似乎都有锚点，最常见的是 LLaMA 3.3，在 2025 年中期左右有密集的预测。像这样的锚点可以使截止后的日期预测看起来更准确。日期集群还表明模型可能存在时间偏差。Figure 9. Visualization of aggregated date predictions across models. Importantly, many models seem to have anchors, most prevalent in LLaMA 3.3, with dense predictions around mid-2025. Anchors like this can cause date predictions after a cutoff to seem more accurate. The clusters of dates also demonstrate how models may be temporally biased.

Computed from a 2×2 confusion matrix where original binary questions have ground-truth是的Yes（贡献 TP/FN）和否定翻转的扰动问题具有基本事实(contributing TP/FN) and negation-flipped perturbed questions have ground-truthNo（贡献 TN/FP）。巴尔。 Acc.：平衡准确度=[1][Prec.][:][TP / (TP + FP).][ Recall][:][TPR = TP / (TP + FN)（准确度] 2[(TPR + TNR); 机会 = 0.50.] 原题）。规格：TNR = TN / (TN + FP)（扰动问题的准确性）。 F1-是/F1-否：每个类别 F1。宏F1：F1-Yes和F1-No的未加权平均值；机会 ≈ 0.50。 MCC：马修斯相关系数； 0 = 随机，1 = 完美。型号按 Macro F1 排序（最高的在前）。粗体：每列最佳。(contributing TN/FP). MCC: Matthews Correlation Coefficient; 0 = random, 1 = perfect. Models sorted by Macro F1 (highest first). Bold: best per column.

D.6 外部基准饱和度比较D.6 External Benchmark Saturation Comparison

为了了解 CUSP 的饱和水平，我们将前沿模型性能与几个广泛使用的科学和推理基准进行比较，包括 MMLU-Pro、GPQA Diamond 和 MedQA。与 MMLU-Pro、GPQA Diamond 和 MedQA 的基准饱和度比较是使用来自人工分析评估和官方 MMLU-Pro Hugging Face 排行榜的公开报告的前沿模型评估结果构建的。To contextualize the saturation level of CUSP, we compare frontier-model performance against several widely used scientific and reasoning benchmarks, including MMLU-Pro, GPQA Diamond, and MedQA. Benchmark saturation comparisons against MMLU-Pro, GPQA Diamond, and MedQA were constructed using publicly reported frontier-model evaluation results from Artificial Analysis Evaluations and the official MMLU-Pro Hugging Face leaderboard.

E 人类评估详情E Human Evaluation Details

E.1 数据集验证的人工评估E.1 Human evaluation of Dataset Validation

我们招募了 10 名人类评估员，主要是研究生水平的研究人员，其隶属机构包括牛津大学、耶鲁大学、密歇根大学、芝加哥大学和香港中文大学（深圳），专业知识涵盖人工智能、材料科学和化学。We recruited 10 human evaluators, primarily graduate-level researchers, with affiliations including the University of Oxford, Yale University, the University of Michigan, the University of Chicago, and CUHK-Shenzhen, and with expertise spanning artificial intelligence, materials science, and chemistry.

我们托管了一个公共网站，并向人类评估人员提供了论文摘要以及基准测试中的问题。然后，根据与 LLM 法官完全相同的标准（参见 F.2），他们被给予保留或从基准中删除问题的二元选择。我们发现，平均而言，LLM 法官在删除本应删除的示例方面实际上更加严格We hosted a public site and gave human evaluators the paper abstracts, alongside the questions in our benchmark. Then, under the exact same criteria as the LLM judge (see F.2), they were given the binary choice to keep or remove the questions from the benchmark. We found that on average the LLM judges were actually more rigorous in removing examples that should have been removed

48

遵循标准，同时保持问题干净、可验证的结果。请参阅 F.3 中的经验示例。under the criteria, while keeping questions with clean, verifiable results. See empirical examples in F.3.

E.2 LLM法官的人工评估E.2 Human Evaluation of LLM Judge

我们使用 60 个示例对 CUSP 的子集进行了人工评估，由三名评估员（两名计算机科学博士和一名博士后学者）进行。所有评估人员都配有一个 Web 界面（图 12）。人类评估者评估 GPT-4o 和 GPT-OSS 的示例。为了公平起见，人类评估者获得了与法学硕士法官完全相同的评分标准。We set up a human evaluation on a subset of CUSP using 60 examples, conducted by three evaluators (two Computer Science PhDs and one postdoctoral scholar). All evaluators were provided with a web interface (Figure 12). The human evaluators evaluate examples across GPT-4o and GPT-OSS. The human evaluators were given the exact same grading rubric as the LLM judge for fairness.

在所有 60 个带注释的对中，人工智能法官实现了皮尔逊相关性：Across all 60 annotated pairs, the AI judge achieves a Pearson correlation ofr = 0 . 34 ( p < 0 .01) 和斯皮尔曼等级相关性01) and a Spearman rank correlation ofρ = 0 .人类 FRQ 分数为 33，平均绝对误差 (MAE) 为 033 with human FRQ scores, with a mean absolute error (MAE) of 0.0-10 分制，共 75 分（图 13）。 Bland-Altman 分析揭示了 +0 的小正偏差75 points on a 0–10 scale (Figure 13). The Bland–Altman analysis reveals a small positive bias of +0.26 分，表明人工智能法官平均比人类评估者稍微慷慨一些，95% 的一致限度跨越 [−126 points, indicating that the AI judge is, on average, marginally more generous than human evaluators, with 95% limits of agreement spanning [−1. 65 , +2 .17]点。这些数字表明了统计上显着且具有实际意义的一致性，其中人工智能法官在完全自动操作的情况下捕获了人类质量判断的广泛顺序结构，尽管仍然存在重要的项目级别差异。17] points. These figures indicate statistically significant and practically meaningful agreement, where the AI judge captures the broad ordinal structure of human quality judgments while operating fully automatically, though non-trivial item-level variance remains.

图 12. 我们的人工评估界面旨在评估一致性、新颖性、可行性和特异性，并将这些判断与 LLM 判断的一致性（通过网络搜索）进行比较。人类评估员和法学硕士法官都可以使用相同的标准。Figure 12. Our human evaluation interface is designed to assess alignment, novelty, feasibility, and specificity, and to compare these judgments against the consistency of the LLM judge (with web search). Both human evaluators and the LLM judge have access to the same rubric.

F.2 验证示例和提示F.2 Verification Examples and Prompts

尖峰过滤下被拒绝的摘要Rejected Abstract under CUSP Filtering

功能梯度促进大象胡须的触觉感知Functional gradients facilitate tactile sensing in elephant whiskers科学Science，2026 年 2 月 |领域：生物学 | doi:10.1126/science.adx8981 |被拒绝, Feb 2026 | Domain: Biology | doi:10.1126/science.adx8981 | Rejected

摘要（摘录）：Abstract (excerpt):

角蛋白复合材料使动物能够用蹄子行走、用羽毛飞行、用皮肤感知。哺乳动物的胡须是细长的角蛋白棒，附着在触觉皮肤结构上，扩大了动物的感觉体积。我们调查了亚洲象身上的胡须（Keratin composites enable animals to hike with hooves, fly with feathers, and sense with skin. Mammalian whiskers are elongated keratin rods attached to tactile skin structures that extend the animal’s sensory volume. We investigated the whiskers that cover Asian elephant (大象Elephas maximus）树干，发现它们在几何和机械上进行了定制，通过对触须基部感受到的振动触觉信号的幅度和频率编码接触位置来促进触觉感知。象须从装甲的躯干皮肤中伸出，从厚实的、圆形的、多孔的、坚硬的底部转变为薄的、卵形的、致密的、柔软的尖端。这些几何形状、孔隙率和刚度的功能梯度独立地调节象鼻触摸的神经力学，以促进高度灵巧的操作，同时确保胡须的耐用性。 [58]) trunks and found that they are geometrically and mechanically tailored to facilitate tactile perception by encoding contact location in the amplitude and frequency of the vibrotactile signal felt at the whisker base. Elephant whiskers emerge from armored trunk skin and shift from a thick, circular, porous, stiff base to a thin, ovular, dense, soft tip. These functional gradients of geometry, porosity, and stiffness independently tune the neuromechanics of elephant trunk touch to facilitate highly dexterous manipulation while ensuring whisker durability. [58]

拒绝原因Reason for rejection gpt-4o-mini：摘要讨论了象须的结构和功能，但没有提供具体的实验结果或可测量的生物量。: The abstract discusses the structure and function of elephant whiskers but does not provide a concrete experimental result or measurable biological quantity.

忠诚验证器系统提示Faithfulness Validator System Prompt

提示：Prompt:

您是一位细心的科学基准验证者。您的任务是判断二元预测语句是否忠实于源摘要/结果文本。仅使用提供的文本。不要使用外部知识。You are a careful scientific benchmark validator. Your task is to judge whether a binary forecasting statement is faithful to the source abstract/result text. Use only the supplied text. Do not use outside knowledge.

您要检查的内容：What you are checking:

• 二进制陈述是否保留与摘要/结果文本相同的科学主张• whether the binary statement preserves the same scientific claim as the abstract/result text

51

  • 是否改变数字、实体、基准、条件、阈值、范围或结果whether it changes a number, entity, benchmark, condition, threshold, scope, or outcome

  • • 是否引入了来源不支持的主张• whether it introduces a claim not supported by the source

  • 重要规则：Important rules:

  • 法官Judge仅only二进制语句本身the binary statement itself

  • Do 不not判断日期/时间措辞——日期是外部生成的，这里应该被忽略judge the date/time wording — the date is generated externally and should be ignored here

  • • 如果该陈述忠实地重述了来源声明，则通过• Pass if the statement is a faithful restatement of the source claim

  • 如果语句改变了含义或发明了不受支持的细节，则失败Fail if the statement changes the meaning or invents unsupported details

  • 严格要求，但不要惩罚外部插入的日期Be strict about the claim, but do not penalise the externally inserted date

  • 评分指南：Scoring guide:

  • 5 — 与来源声明完全或几乎完全匹配5 — exact or nearly exact match to the source claim

  • 4 — 细微的措辞差异，但仍然忠实4 — minor wording differences, but still faithful

  • 3 — 部分忠实/边缘3 — partially faithful / borderline

  • 2 - 大多不受支持或有意义的改变2 — mostly unsupported or meaningfully altered

  • 1——明显不忠1 — clearly unfaithful

仅返回具有此架构的有效 JSON：Return only valid JSON with this schema: { "verdict": "pass | fail | unclear", "score": 1-5, "reason": "short explanation", "mismatch[_] types": ["numbers","entity","condition", "outcome","time","scope", "threshold","none"] }

可验证性验证器系统提示Verifiability Validator System Prompt

提示：Prompt:

您是一位细心的科学基准验证者。你的任务是判断二进制陈述是否足够具体以进行客观验证。仅使用提供的文本。不要使用外部知识。这是You are a careful scientific benchmark validator. Your task is to judge whether the binary statement is concrete enough to be objectively verified. Use only the supplied text. Do not use outside knowledge. This is不not写作质量检查——如果基本主张含糊不清，一个句子可能听起来不错，但仍然失败。您要检查的内容：a writing-quality check — a sentence can sound fine and still fail if the underlying claim is vague. What you are checking:

  • 该陈述是否描述了具体的科学主张whether the statement describes a concrete scientific claim

  • 第三方是否可以在不猜测的情况下决定是/否whether a third party could decide yes/no without guessing

  • 该主张是否含糊、不明确或过于解释性whether the claim is vague, underspecified, or too interpretive

重要规则：Important rules:

  • 整体判断索赔Judge the claim as a whole

  • Do 不not仅仅因为措辞使用比较语言而拒绝reject simply because the wording uses comparison language

  • 仅当比较或声明的可操作性不足以进行检查时才拒绝Reject only if the comparison or claim is not operationalised enough to be checked

  • 如果声明足够具体，可以从来源客观地验证，则通过Pass if the claim is specific enough to be objectively verifiable from the source

  • 如果模糊的话就会失败——例如“更好”、“好”、“强”、“有效”或“可比较”——没有明确的衡量标准Fail if it is vague — e.g. “better”, “good”, “strong”, “effective”, or “comparable” — without a clear measurable criterion

通行证示例：Examples of pass:

  • “MMLU 的准确率达到 72.4%”“achieves 72.4% accuracy on MMLU”

  • “在规定的基准上将 F1 从 81.2 提高到 84.0”“improves F1 from 81.2 to 84.0 on the stated benchmark”

  • “在指定的评估协议下，错误率降低了 20%”“reduces error rate by 20% under the specified evaluation protocol”

52

失败的例子：Examples of fail:

  • 当消息来源未定义如何衡量可比性时，“在推理任务上实现与 OpenAI-o1-1217 相当的性能”“achieves performance comparable to OpenAI-o1-1217 on reasoning tasks” when the source does not define how comparability is measured

  • “提高性能”，没有指标、基准或阈值“improves performance” with no metric, benchmark, or threshold

  • “比以前的方法更好”，没有客观标准“better than previous methods” with no objective criterion

评分指南：Scoring guide:

  • 5——完全具体、客观、易于验证5 — fully specific, objective, and easy to verify

  • 4——大部分具体，有轻微的模糊性4 — mostly specific with minor ambiguity

  • 3 — 临界/部分可测试3 — borderline / partly testable

  • 2 — 大多含糊或不明确2 — mostly vague or underspecified

  • 1——无法客观验证1 — not objectively verifiable

仅返回具有此架构的有效 JSON：Return only valid JSON with this schema: 

{
"verdict":"pass|fail|unclear",
"score":1-5,
"reason":"shortexplanation"
}

扰动验证器系统提示Perturbation Validator System Prompt

提示：Prompt:

您是一位细心的科学基准验证者。你的任务是判断扰动的二元问题是否是真正的扰动。仅使用提供的文本。不要使用外部知识。You are a careful scientific benchmark validator. Your task is to judge whether the perturbed binary question is a genuine perturbation. Use only the supplied text. Do not use outside knowledge.

您要检查的内容：What you are checking:

  • 受到干扰的问题是否改变了原始问题的显着细节whether the perturbed question changes a salient detail from the original question

  • 该更改是否有意义地破坏了源摘要/结果文本的支持whether that change meaningfully breaks support from the source abstract/result text

  • 这个令人不安的问题是否不仅仅是一个释义或琐碎的改写whether the perturbed question is not merely a paraphrase or trivial rewording

重要规则：Important rules:

  • 仅当扰动改变了有意义的方面（例如数字、阈值、实体、结果、时间、范围或条件）时才通过Pass only if the perturbation changes a meaningful aspect such as number, threshold, entity, outcome, time, scope, or condition

  • 仅当源不再直接支持受干扰的版本时才通过Pass only if the perturbed version is no longer directly supported by the source

  • 如果本质上是同一个问题，只是修饰性的措辞发生了变化，则失败Fail if it is essentially the same question with cosmetic wording changes

  • 如果没有对来源声明提出真正的挑战，则失败Fail if it does not introduce a real challenge to the source claim

  • 评分指南：Scoring guide:

  • 5——强烈的、明显改变的扰动5 — strong, clearly altered perturbation

  • 4 — 有效的扰动，但有轻微的歧义4 — valid perturbation with minor ambiguity

  • 3 — 边界/弱扰动3 — borderline / weak perturbation

  • 2 — 可能不是真正的扰动2 — likely not a real perturbation

  • 1 — 显然没有受到干扰1 — clearly not perturbed

仅返回具有此架构的有效 JSON：Return only valid JSON with this schema: 

{
"verdict":"pass|fail|unclear",
"score":1-5,
"reason":"shortexplanation",

53

  • "changed[_] elements": ["numbers","entity","condition", "outcome","time","scope", "threshold","none"]

  • }

MCQ 词干验证器MCQ Stem Validator

  • 提示：您是一位严格的科学基准验证者。您的任务只是评估 MCQ 问题陈述。您必须检查两件事：Prompt: You are a strict scientific benchmark validator. Your task is to evaluate the MCQ problem statement only. You must check two things:

  • 忠实性：问题陈述是否准确反映了源摘要？Faithfulness: does the problem statement accurately reflect the source abstract?

  • 可验证性：问题陈述是否足够具体，可以从源头客观地回答？Verifiability: is the problem statement concrete enough to be objectively answered from the source?

重要规则：Important rules:

  • 仅判断 MCQ 主干/问题陈述 - 不判断本次通话中的答案选择Judge only the MCQ stem/problem statement — do not judge the answer choices in this call

  • 忽略任何预测措辞。主干经常会问“到 2026 年 3 月，哪种方法最有可能实现 X？”之类的问题。 ——完全忽略日期、可能性预测框架和推测时间表；做Ignore any forecasting wording. The stem often asks things like “by March 2026, which approach is most likely to achieve X?” — completely ignore the date, the likelihood-prediction framing, and speculative timelines; do不not惩罚询问未来的茎penalise the stem for asking about the future

  • 仅评估问题的科学核心是否准确反映了摘要的方法和结果Assess only whether the scientific core of the question accurately reflects the abstract’s methodology and results

  • 如果科学核心改变了主张、引入了不受支持的细节或错误表述了摘要，则失败Fail if the scientific core changes the claim, introduces unsupported details, or misstates the abstract

  • 如果科学核心模糊、不明确或不可操作，则失败Fail if the scientific core is vague, underspecified, or not operationalisable

  • 仅当核心科学主张既忠实又具体时才能通过Pass only if the core scientific claim is both faithful and concrete

  • 失败的例子：Examples of fail:

  • 词干添加了非抽象的基准、指标或阈值the stem adds a benchmark, metric, or threshold not in the abstract

  • 词干使用模糊的语言，例如“提高绩效”，没有可衡量的标准the stem uses vague language such as “improve performance” with no measurable criterion

  • 词干询问无法仅从摘要中验证的主张the stem asks about a claim that cannot be verified from the abstract alone

  • 评分指南：Scoring guide:

  • 5 — 完全忠实且可明确验证5 — fully faithful and clearly verifiable

  • 4——大部分都很强，但有轻微的模糊性4 — mostly strong with minor ambiguity

  • 3——边界3 — borderline

  • 2——弱2 — weak

  • 1 — 明显无效1 — clearly invalid

仅返回具有此架构的有效 JSON：Return only valid JSON with this schema: 

{
"verdict":"pass|fail|unclear",
"score":1-5,
"reason":"shortexplanation",
"issue[_]types":["faithfulness","verifiability","none"]
}

MCQ 答案验证器MCQ Answer Validator

提示：您是一位严格的科学基准验证者。您的任务只是评估标记的正确答案选项。您必须检查源摘要是否支持所选答案作为正确的技术方法、机制或结果。重要规则：Prompt: You are a strict scientific benchmark validator. Your task is to evaluate the marked correct answer choice only. You must check whether the selected answer is supported by the source abstract as the correct technical approach, mechanism, or result. Important rules:

  • 仅判断标记的正确答案选项 - 不要判断本次通话中的干扰因素Judge only the marked correct answer choice — do not judge distractors in this call

54

  • 仅当摘要支持或明确暗示答案选择时才通过Pass only if the answer choice is supported or clearly implied by the abstract

  • 如果答案选择不受支持、不匹配或发明了摘要中不存在的机制，则失败Fail if the answer choice is unsupported, mismatched, or invents a mechanism not present in the abstract

  • 如果摘要为多个答案提供了足够的证据，请解释其中的歧义If the abstract gives enough evidence for more than one answer, explain the ambiguity

  • 评分指南：Scoring guide:

  • 5 — 正确且直接支持5 — correct and directly supported

  • 4 — 支持，但有较小的解释差距4 — supported with minor interpretive gap

  • 3——边界3 — borderline

  • 2 — 可能不正确2 — likely incorrect

  • 1——明显错误1 — clearly wrong

仅返回具有此架构的有效 JSON：Return only valid JSON with this schema: { "verdict": "pass | fail | unclear", "score": 1-5, "reason": "short explanation", "issue[_] types": ["unsupported[_] answer","ambiguous[_] answer","none"] }

MCQ 干扰器验证器MCQ Distractor Validator

提示：Prompt:

您是一位严格的科学基准验证者。您的任务只是评估不正确的答案选项。您必须检查干扰因素是否：You are a strict scientific benchmark validator. Your task is to evaluate the incorrect answer choices only. You must check whether the distractors are:

  1. 足够合理，需要真正的推理，plausible enough to require real reasoning,

  2. 没有摘要的直接支持，not directly supported by the abstract,

  3. 并非微不足道的错误或明显被消除。not trivially wrong or obviously eliminated.

重要规则：Important rules:

  • 将干扰因素作为一个集合进行判断——不要判断此调用中的词干或正确答案Judge the distractors as a set — do not judge the stem or the correct answer in this call

  • 仅当干扰因素不平凡且足够合理时才通过Pass only if the distractors are non-trivial and sufficiently plausible

  • 如果干扰因素太容易、太明显错误或直接由摘要支持，则失败Fail if the distractors are too easy, too obviously wrong, or directly supported by the abstract

  • 如果干扰因素与正确答案没有有意义的竞争，则失败Fail if the distractors are not meaningfully competitive with the correct answer

  • 评分指南：Scoring guide:

  • 5——强烈的干扰因素，高度可信5 — strong distractors, highly plausible

  • 4 — 有小问题的良好干扰因素4 — good distractors with minor issues

  • 3——边界3 — borderline

  • 2 — 弱干扰因素2 — weak distractors

  • 1 — 琐碎或明显不好的干扰因素1 — trivial or obviously bad distractors

仅返回具有此架构的有效 JSON：Return only valid JSON with this schema:

{
"verdict":"pass|fail|unclear",
"score":1-5,
"reason":"shortexplanation",
"issue[_]types":["too[_]easy","unsupported[_]by[_]abstract",
"not[_]plausible","none"]
}

55

FRQ 验证器系统提示FRQ Validator System Prompt

提示：Prompt:

您是一位严格的科学基准验证者。您的任务是评估自由回答问题 (FRQ) 提示。 FRQ 通常从建立“背景”或“问题陈述”开始（例如，“考虑到推理质量代理的不可靠性……”）。然后它要求用户提出一个解决方案（例如，“在[日期]之前提出一个具体的方法……”）。您的主要目标是评估是否You are a strict scientific benchmark validator. Your task is to evaluate the Free Response Question (FRQ) prompt. The FRQ usually begins by establishing a “background” or “problem statement” (e.g., “Given the unreliability of proxies for reasoning quality. . . ”). Then it asks the user to propose a solution (e.g., “propose a concrete method. . . by [Date]”). Your primary goal is to evaluate whether the背景前提/问题陈述background premise / problem statementFRQ 中建立的内容准确且忠实于源摘要。established in the FRQ is accurate and faithful to the source abstract.

重要规则：Important rules:

  • 提取 FRQ 中嵌入的前提/背景陈述Extract the premise/background statement embedded in the FRQ

  • 直接将此前提与源摘要进行比较Compare this premise directly against the source abstract

  • 摘要是否确实描述了这个具体问题、挑战或背景情况？Does the abstract actually describe this specific problem, challenge, or background context?

  • 如果 FRQ 发明了问题、歪曲了挑战或与摘要的框架相矛盾，则失败Fail if the FRQ invents a problem, misrepresents the challenge, or contradicts the abstract’s framing

  • 如果问题陈述/背景忠实于摘要且准确，则通过Pass if the problem statement/background is faithful and accurate to the abstract

  • 忽略任何预测措辞——不要因为 FRQ 要求“到 2026 年 3 月”或任何其他日期提供预测或解决方案而受到惩罚；这是预期和要求的Ignore any forecasting wording — do not penalise the FRQ for asking for a prediction or solution “by March 2026” or any other date; that is expected and required

  • 不要评估 FRQ 的“可衡量性”或它是否包含特定基准（与二进制或 MCQ 不同）——这是一个自由回答问题，因此要求“具体方法”或“实施计划”的开放式措辞正是我们想要的Do not evaluate the FRQ for “measurability” or whether it includes specific benchmarks (unlike binary or MCQs) — this is a free-response question, so open-ended phrasing asking for a “concrete method” or “implementation plan” is exactly what we want

  • 评分指南：Scoring guide:

  • 5——问题陈述完全忠实于摘要5 — problem statement is perfectly faithful to the abstract

  • 4 — 大部分忠实，语义差异较小4 — mostly faithful, minor semantic differences

  • 3——边界3 — borderline

  • 2——与摘要实际问题的联系较弱2 — weak connection to the abstract’s actual problem

  • 1 — 明显不忠实，发明了一个并非抽象的问题1 — clearly unfaithful, invents a problem not in the abstract

仅返回具有此架构的有效 JSON：Return only valid JSON with this schema: 

{
"verdict":"pass|fail|unclear",
"score":1-5,
"reason":"shortexplanation",
"issue[_]types":["unfaithful[_]problem[_]statement","none"]
}

F.3 基准验证中的人类与人工智能F.3 Human Vs AI in Benchmark Validation

项目 1 • 二进制Item 1 • Binary

（二进制）(Binary)

到 2024 年 12 月，一种方法能否将思想链推理中的代币成本降低 30%，同时在评估的任务中保持性能下降不超过 2%？By December 2024, will a method reduce token costs in Chain-of-Thought reasoning by 30% while maintaining a performance reduction of less than 2% across evaluated tasks?

× AI 删除·人类保留 二进制声明引入了源文本不支持的具体数字声明（代币成本降低 30%，性能降低不到 2%），源文本仅提到代币成本总体降低，性能略有降低。AI Removed · Human Kept The binary statement introduces specific numerical claims (30% token cost reduction and less than 2% performance reduction) that are not supported by the source text, which only mentions a general reduction in token costs with a slight performance reduction.

56

项目 2 • 二进制Item 2 • Binary

（二进制）(Binary)

到 2024 年 6 月，一种方法能否在多模式基准测试中实现最先进的性能？By June 2024, will a method achieve state-of-the-art performance on multimodal benchmarks?

× AI被移除·人类被保留AI Removed · Human Kept

如果没有源文本中定义的具体指标、基准或阈值，实现“多模式基准上最先进的性能”的说法是模糊的，并且无法客观验证。The claim of achieving ‘state-of-the-art performance on multimodal benchmarks’ is vague and not objectively verifiable without specific metrics, benchmarks, or thresholds defined in the source text.

项目 3 • MCQItem 3 • MCQ

（MCQ）(MCQ)

考虑到在不显着影响准确性的情况下减少推理任务中代币使用的挑战，以下哪种方法最有可能在 2024 年 12 月之前实现代币成本降低 30%？Given the challenge of reducing token usage in reasoning tasks without significantly compromising accuracy, which of the following approaches is most likely to achieve a 30% reduction in token costs by December 2024?

× AI被移除·人类被保留AI Removed · Human Kept

该词干引入了“代币成本降低 30%”的特定阈值，但摘要中并未提及或支持这一阈值。这一添加使得问题不忠实于原始材料。The stem introduces a specific threshold of ‘30% reduction in token costs’ which is not mentioned or supported in the abstract. This addition makes the question unfaithful to the source material.

✓ 人类被移除·人工智能被保留Human Removed · AI Kept

该声明是具体的，参考了具体基准 (MMIU)，其准确度为 55.7%，具有明确、可测量的阈值，使其客观可验证。The claim is specific, referencing a concrete benchmark (MMIU) with a clear, measurable threshold of 55.7% accuracy, making it objectively verifiable.

✓ 人类被移除·人工智能被保留Human Removed · AI Kept

The focus on performance in speech translation and AIR-Bench tasks with reduced training data is faithful to the abstract’s claims about Soundwave’s efficiency and results. The question is concrete enough to be objectively answered based on the abstract’s content.

系统提示：严谨科学的评判System Prompt: Rigorous Scientific Evaluation Judge

您是一位严谨科学的评价法官。您的工作是评估 LLM RESPONSE（由You are a rigorous scientific evaluation judge. Your job is to assess the LLM RESPONSE (delimited by«LLM[_] RESPONSE»用户消息中的标签）对照真实参考（这些标签之外的所有内容）。切勿将网络搜索返回的内容与 LLM 所写的内容混淆 - LLM 响应只是其中的文本tags in the user message) against the GROUND-TRUTH REFERENCE (everything outside those tags). Never confuse what the web search returns with what the LLM wrote — the LLM RESPONSE is ONLY the text inside«LLM[_] RESPONSE» .

使用WEB搜索查找实际论文，验证真实的方法论，并检查里面是否有声明USE WEB SEARCH to look up the actual paper, verify the real methodology, and check whether claims inside«LLM[_] RESPONSE»是准确的。使用搜索结果作为事实真相——而不是确认法学硕士。are accurate. Use search results as ground truth — not to confirm the LLM.

  • === 第 1 部分：FRQ 评分 — 使用严格的锚 ====== PART 1: FRQ SCORING — use strict anchors ===

1. 一致性（0-10）：LLM RESPONSE 是否描述了论文中使用的具体方法？使用网络搜索来查找实际的纸质方法。1. alignment (0–10): Does the LLM RESPONSE describe the specific approach used in the paper? Use web search to find the actual paper method.

  • 0-2：方向完全错误或没有有意义的内容0–2: completely wrong direction or no meaningful content

  • 3-4：大致正确的区域，但缺少实际方法的关键细节3–4: roughly right area but missing key specifics of the actual method

  • 5-6：抓住了主要思想，但缺乏重要细节或错误表述了它们5–6: captures the main idea but lacks important details or misstates them

  • 7-8：核心技术匹配且大多数关键细节正确7–8: matches the core technique with most key details correct

  • 9-10：精确匹配，包括具体的设计选择和实施9–10: precise match including specific design choices and implementation

2. 特异性 (0–10)：LLM RESPONSE 在技术上是否具体？2. specificity (0–10): Is the LLM RESPONSE technically concrete?

  • 0-2：纯粹的流行语或单句模糊的主张0–2: pure buzzwords or single-sentence vague claims

  • 3-4：命名一种技术，但没有解释如何应用它3–4: names a technique but no explanation of how it is applied

  • 5-6：从概念层面解释该方法5–6: explains the method at a conceptual level

  • 7-8：提供实现级别的详细信息（架构、损失、数据）7–8: provides implementation-level details (architecture, loss, data)

  • 9-10：可直接实施的完整技术方案9–10: full technical recipe that could be directly implemented

58

3. 新颖性 (0–10)：法学硕士的回答是否显示出不明显的见解？ 0-2：重申该问题领域最明显的基线 3-4：提出对标准基线的微小、明显的变化 5-6：超越明显，但洞察力在该领域是众所周知的3. novelty (0–10): Does the LLM RESPONSE show non-obvious insight? 0–2: restates the most obvious baseline for this problem area 3–4: proposes minor, obvious variations on standard baselines 5–6: goes beyond obvious but the insight is well-known in the field

  • 7-8：提出一些重要且技术上合理的建议7–8: proposes something non-trivial and technically justified

  • 9-10：高度原创且技术上合理的突破性见解9–10: highly original and technically justified breakthrough insight

4. 可行性（0-10）：考虑到现实世界的限制，所提出的方法实际上可行吗？ 0-2：明显不可行（不存在的方法，不可能的假设） 3-4：主要可行性问题（不切实际的计算/数据或不受支持的主张） 5-6：看似合理，但存在显着的实际问题4. feasibility (0–10): Is the proposed approach actually feasible given real-world constraints? 0–2: clearly infeasible (non-existent methods, impossible assumptions) 3–4: major feasibility issues (unrealistic compute/data or unsupported claims) 5–6: plausible but with notable practical concerns

  • 7-8：基本可行，但有一些小注意事项7–8: largely feasible with minor caveats

9-10：明显可行且与现实世界的实施一致9–10: clearly feasible and consistent with real-world implementations Respond with a “‘json代码块。写 1-2 个带有内联引用的推理句子（code block. Write 1-2 sentences of reasoning with inline citations ( [title](url)）在块之前。) before the block. { "alignment": < 0-10 > , "specificity": < 0-10 > , "novelty": < 0-10 > , "feasibility": < 0-10 > , "rationale": " < 2-3 sentence summary explaining the scores for alignment, specificity, novelty, and feasibility > " }

您是科学预测基准的数据污染和泄漏审核员。该基准测试模型是否可以预测未来的科学结果。每个问题都与模型知识截止后发布的发现有关。该论文的发表日期是You are a data-contamination and leakage auditor for a scientific forecasting benchmark. The benchmark tests whether models can predict future scientific results. Each question is about a discovery published AFTER the model’s knowledge cutoff. The paper’s publication date is{pub[_] date} .

======你唯一的工作============ YOUR ONLY JOB ======

决定 LLM RESPONSE 是否（由Decide whether the LLM RESPONSE (delimited by «LLM[_] RESPONSE»用户消息中的标签）明确命名特定的截止后实体。tags in the user message) explicitly names a specific post-cutoff entity.

按顺序执行以下步骤 — 不要跳过：FOLLOW THESE STEPS IN ORDER — DO NOT SKIP:

逐字抄出出现在各部分之间的每个专有名词、模型名称、论文标题、系统名称或数据集名称Copy out every proper noun, model name, paper title, system name, or dataset name that appears VERBATIM between the «LLM[_] RESPONSE»标签。如果您发现零个此类名称，请立即返回 verdict='pass' — 不要执行任何网络搜索。tags. 3. 第 3 步 — 决定：3. STEP 3 — DECIDE:

使用金属和 MXene 薄膜的电磁干扰屏蔽 [59]Electromagnetic interference shielding using metal and MXene thin films [59]自然Nature，2025 年 10 月 |领域：材料科学| doi:10.1016/j.cell.2026.01.023, Oct 2025 | Domain: Materials Science | doi:10.1016/j.cell.2026.01.023

到2025年10月，是否有一种方法能够在1层厚度下实现约70分贝的电磁干扰屏蔽By October 2025, will a method achieve electromagnetic interference shielding of about 70 decibels at a thickness of 1µm厚度为1.9时约80分贝and about 80 decibels at a thickness of 1.9µm，展示与便携式 USB 3.0 闪存驱动器和灵活肖特基二极管的兼容性？, demonstrating compatibility with portable USB 3.0 flash drives and flexible Schottky diodes?

60

答案：是的Answer: Yes

二元问题（困惑）：Binary question (perturbed):

到 2025 年 10 月，是否有一种方法能够在 1 时实现 75 dB 的电磁干扰屏蔽By October 2025, will a method achieve electromagnetic interference shielding of 75 dB at 1µm1.9 时为 85 dBand 85 dB at 1.9µm ?

答案：没有Answer: No

扰动原理：添加了明确的未满足约束，要求 1 µm 处为 75 dB，1.9 µm 处为 85 dB。因此，根据 CUSP 验证，索赔无效。Perturbation rationale: Added a definitive unmet constraint requiring 75 dB at 1 µm and 85 dB at 1.9 µm. Therefore rendering the claim invalid under CUSP verification.

技术 MCQ：统一生物分子建模Technical MCQ: Unified Biomolecular Modeling

Ground Truth Discovery：AlphaFold 3 [9] 目标日期：2024 年 5 月Ground Truth Discovery: AlphaFold 3 [9] Target Date: May 2024

链接：Link:https://doi.org/10.1038/s41586-024-07487-w

问题：考虑到跨不同复合物的碎片生物分子相互作用建模的挑战，哪种方法最有可能在 2024 年 5 月之前实现蛋白质-配体、蛋白质-核酸和抗体-抗原预测的更高准确度？Question: Given the challenge of fragmented biomolecular interaction modeling across diverse complexes, which approach is most likely to achieve far greater accuracy for protein-ligand, protein-nucleic acid, and antibody-antigen predictions by May 2024?

  • A) 基于扩散的架构通过跨生物分子复合物的迭代细化和概率采样对关节结构进行建模。A) Diffusion-based architecture modeling joint structures with iterative refinement and probabilistic sampling across biomolecular complexes.

  • B）图神经网络将空间和化学特征与分层关注相结合以进行交互预测。B) Graph neural networks integrating spatial and chemical features with hierarchical attention for interaction prediction.

  • C) 基于能量的模型使用不同生物分子相互作用类型的模拟退火来优化结合亲和力。C) Energy-based models optimizing binding affinity using simulated annealing across diverse biomolecular interaction types.

  • D）基于 Transformer 的架构，利用序列和结构嵌入来进行统一的生物分子相互作用预测。D) Transformer-based architecture leveraging sequence and structural embeddings for unified biomolecular interaction predictions.

正确答案：A. 基于扩散的关节结构建模（如 AlphaFold 3 中所实现的那样）是唯一能够按照所描述的规模和精度直接解决统一生物分子复合体预测的方法。Correct answer: A. Diffusion-based joint structure modeling — as realised in AlphaFold 3 — is the only approach that directly addresses unified biomolecular complex prediction at the scale and accuracy described.

I 基准创建标准I Benchmark Creation Criteria

系统提示System Prompt

您是 {info['name']} 域的科学论文筛选者。You are a scientific paper screener for the {info[’name’]} domain.

给定一篇论文摘要，确定它是否包含至少一个具体的、可验证的结果或符合以下任何标准的突破：Given a paper abstract, determine if it contains AT LEAST ONE concrete, verifiable result or breakthrough matching any of these criteria:

{标准文本}{criteria_text}

如果摘要描述了具有具体方法和可验证结果的明显突破（例如，“实现 X 的第一个方法”、“在 Y 基准上优于所有先前的方法”），则摘要通过。纯粹描述性、推测性或类似评论的摘要，没有具体结果或方法，不合格。仅用一行回复：An abstract passes if it describes a clear breakthrough with a concrete method and a verifiable outcome (e.g., ‘first method to achieve X’, ‘outperforms all prior methods on Y benchmark’). FAIL abstracts that are purely descriptive, speculative, or review-like with no concrete result or method. Reply with exactly one line:

通过：PASS:<一句话概括找到的具体结果one-sentence summary of the concrete result found>

or

失败：FAIL:<一句话原因没有找到具体结果one-sentence reason why no concrete result was found>

纳入标准摘要过滤Inclusion Criteria Abstract Filtering

人工智能Artificial Intelligence

  • 使用清晰的方法或途径描述具体的技术突破，并在公认的基准、竞赛或评估（例如 CASP、MATH、ImageNet、MMLU）上进行验证，即使没有确切的数字分数。Describes a concrete technical breakthrough with a clear method or approach, validated on a recognized benchmark, competition, or evaluation (e.g., CASP, MATH, ImageNet, MMLU), even without exact numeric scores.

  • 报告具体的性能指标（准确度、F1、BLEU、困惑度等）或展示相对于先前方法的可测量的改进。Reports specific performance metrics (accuracy, F1, BLEU, perplexity, etc.) or demonstrates measurable improvement over prior methods.

  • 通过可描述的方法实现明确定义的能力里程碑（例如，“执行 X 的第一种方法”、“在 Y 上匹配或超过人类表现”）。Achieves a clearly defined capability milestone (e.g., ‘first method to do X’, ‘matches or exceeds human performance on Y’) with a describable method.

化学Chemistry

  • 描述具体的合成、反应或材料发现，采用明确的方法产生可验证的结果（新化合物、新反应途径、新材料特性）。Describes a concrete synthesis, reaction, or material discovery with a clear method that produces a verifiable outcome (new compound, new reaction pathway, new material property).

  • 报告可测量的数量（产量、选择性、结合亲和力、电导率、速率）或展示相对于先前方法的改进。Reports measurable quantities (yields, selectivity, binding affinity, conductivity, rates) or demonstrates improvement over prior methods.

63

  • 通过可描述的方法实现能力里程碑（例如，“首次合成 X”、“在室温下实现 Y”）。Achieves a capability milestone (e.g., ‘first synthesis of X’, ‘enables Y at room temperature’) with a describable approach.

生物学/生命科学Biology / Life Sciences

  • 描述具体的生物学发现，具有明确的实验方法和可验证的结果（新机制、途径、基因功能、治疗效果）。Describes a concrete biological discovery with a clear experimental method and verifiable outcome (new mechanism, pathway, gene function, therapeutic effect).

  • 报告可测量的生物量（倍数变化、存活率、表达水平、p 值）或展示相对于先前方法的改进。Reports measurable biological quantities (fold changes, survival rates, expression levels, p-values) or demonstrates improvement over prior methods.

  • 通过可描述的实验方法实现能力里程碑（例如，“X 的首次演示”、“确定 Y 背后的机制”）。Achieves a capability milestone (e.g., ‘first demonstration of X’, ‘identifies the mechanism behind Y’) with a describable experimental approach.

物理学Physics

  • 描述具有明确方法和可验证结果（新测量、新现象、新预测）的具体实验或理论突破。Describes a concrete experimental or theoretical breakthrough with a clear method and verifiable outcome (new measurement, new phenomenon, new prediction).

  • 报告可测量的物理量（精度、分辨率、能量尺度、横截面）或展示相对于先前方法的改进。Reports measurable physical quantities (precision, resolution, energy scales, cross-sections) or demonstrates improvement over prior methods.

  • 通过可描述的方法实现能力里程碑（例如，“首次观察 X”、“实现 Y 的相干时间”）。Achieves a capability milestone (e.g., ‘first observation of X’, ‘achieves coherence time of Y’) with a describable approach.

普通科学General Science

  • 描述具有明确方法和可独立复制或验证的可验证结果的具体科学突破或发现。Describes a concrete scientific breakthrough or discovery with a clear method and a verifiable outcome that could be independently reproduced or validated.

  • 报告可衡量的结果或证明比以前的工作有明显的改进，即使是定性描述的（例如，“大大优于其他方法”）。Reports measurable results or demonstrates clear improvement over prior work, even if described qualitatively (e.g., ‘greatly outperforming other methods’).

  • 通过可描述的方法实现定义的能力里程碑，并根据公认的标准、基线或现有技术水平进行验证。Achieves a defined capability milestone with a describable approach, validated against a recognized standard, baseline, or prior state of the art.

提取技术细节Extract Technical Details

您是一位专家研究科学家。读取用户的摘要并准确返回一个 JSON 对象。 JSON 必须具有三个键：“results_and_metrics”、“technical_approach”和“problem_statement”。You are an expert research scientist. Read the user’s abstract and RETURN EXACTLY one JSON object. The JSON must have three keys: ‘results_and_metrics’, ‘technical_approach’, and ‘problem_statement’.

  • “results_and_metrics”：单个句子仅捕获任何科学领域的可测量结果、性能数字、基准结果或展示的能力（例如人工智能准确性、生物活性、物理极限）。您必须包括具体的定量细节、确切的改进百分比、精确的实验条件以及实际的公共基准或实体名称（如果存在于摘要中）。不要写模糊或笼统的总结（例如，不要说“提高准确性”或“提高效率”，而是说“在 X 基准上达到 94.2% 的准确性”、“将蛋白质结合亲和力提高 2 倍”或“合成在 135 K 下具有超导转变的材料”）。不要提及所使用的方法、架构或技术——只提及可验证的结果。在适当的情况下，用“系统”或“方法”替换具体建议的模型或系统名称。重要：不要使用本文中首次引入的任何新颖术语、指标名称或概念（例如“深度思考比率”、“Grokked-Score”）。知识截止之前的模型将不知道这些术语。相反，从功能上描述它们（例如，“进行重大内部修改的代币比例”）。‘results_and_metrics’: A single sentence capturing ONLY the measurable outcomes, performance numbers, benchmark results, or demonstrated capabilities across ANY scientific domain (e.g., AI accuracy, biological activity, physical limits). You MUST include specific quantitative details, exact percentage improvements, precise experimental conditions, and actual public benchmark or entity names if present in the abstract. Do NOT write a vague or generalized summary (e.g., do NOT say ‘improves accuracy’ or ‘increases efficiency’, instead say ‘achieves 94.2% accuracy on X benchmark’, ‘increases protein binding affinity by 2-fold’, or ‘synthesizes a material with a superconducting transition at 135 K’). DO NOT mention the method, architecture, or technique used — only the verifiable outcome. Replace specific proposed model or system names with ‘a system’ or ‘a method’ where appropriate. CRITICAL: Do NOT use any novel terms, metric names, or concepts that are introduced for the first time in this paper (e.g., ‘deep-thinking ratio’, ‘Grokked-Score’). A model from before the knowledge cutoff will not know these terms. Instead, describe them functionally (e.g., ‘the proportion of tokens undergoing significant internal revisions’).

  • “technical_approach”：详细的、技术性的、面向方法的规范，说明如何实现结果。包括具体机制、实验设计、架构转变或‘technical_approach’: A detailed, technical, method-oriented specification of HOW the result was achieved. Include the specific mechanism, experimental design, architectural shift, or

算法创新（例如，“使用稀疏自动编码器来映射内部激活”、“采用高压金刚石砧细胞”或“通过小分子抑制剂靶向 XYZ 通路”）。请勿包含具体建议的型号名称或品牌；将它们替换为“系统”或“方法”。重要：不要包含任何命名技术、命名算法、论文中引入的新术语或首字母缩略词（例如，不要说“GRPO”、“CRISPR”、“NMR”、“LoRA”）。相反，从机械角度描述该技术的作用（例如，用“比较多个输出的组级相对策略优化”代替“GRPO”，或者用“诱导靶向双链断裂的 RNA 引导核酸内切酶系统”代替“CRISPR-Cas9”）。该字段仅供内部答案键使用。algorithmic innovation (e.g., ‘uses sparse autoencoders to map internal activations’, ‘employs a high-pressure diamond anvil cell’, or ‘targets the XYZ pathway via a small-molecule inhibitor’). 创建二元问题Create Binary Question

问题上下文：“{problem_statement}” 实现的结果：“{results_and_metrics}” 目标日期：{ human_date} 编写一个二元（是/否）预测问题。规则：Problem context: “{problem_statement}” Result achieved: “{results_and_metrics}” Target date: {human_date} Write ONE binary (Yes/No) forecasting question. Rules:

  • 问题必须以：“将采用一种方法”或“在{人类日期}之前，将采用一种方法”。The question must begin with: ‘Will a method’ or ‘By {human_date}, will a method’.

  • 准确的语法流利性至关重要。您必须调整“取得结果”中的动词时态以适应句子。EXACT GRAMMATICAL FLUENCY IS CRITICAL. You must adapt the verb tense from ‘Result achieved’ to fit the sentence.

  • 坏例子：一种方法能否实现稳健的正相关......Bad Example: Will a method achieve Achieves a robust positive correlation...

  • – 好例子：一种方法能否实现稳健的正相关......– Good Example: Will a method achieve a robust positive correlation...

  • 坏例子：到 2026 年 3 月，某种方法是否会表现出 10% 的增长……Bad Example: By 2026-03, will a method Demonstrates a 10% increase...

  • 好例子：到 2026 年 3 月，某种方法是否会显示出 10% 的增长......Good Example: By 2026-03, will a method demonstrate a 10% increase...

  • 如果违反语法，请勿盲目复制粘贴。一定要修正动词！Do not blindly copy-paste if it breaks grammar. ALWAYS fix the verb!

  • 如果问题一开始还没有的话，请以“截止到{人类日期}？”结束问题。End the question with ‘by {human_date}?’ if not already at the start.

  • 仅参考可衡量的结果（指标、基准、数字改进或具体能力）。Refer ONLY to measurable results (metrics, benchmarks, numeric improvements, or concrete capabilities).

  • 不要提及任何方法、技术、架构或如何实现结果。Do NOT mention any methods, techniques, architectures, or how the result is achieved.

  • 请勿使用“演示”、“启用”或“通过使用”等词语。Do NOT use words like ‘demonstrate’, ‘enable’, or ‘by using’.

  • 保持一个清晰的句子。Keep it to a single clear sentence.

返回带有键“binary_question”的 JSON。Return JSON with key ‘binary_question’.

创建二元扰动Create Binary Perturb

原始结果声明：“{results_and_metrics}” 问题上下文：“{problem_statement}” 创建此结果声明的听起来合理的反事实替代版本Original result claim: “{results_and_metrics}” Problem context: “{problem_statement}” Create a COUNTERFACTUAL ALTERNATIVE version of this result claim that is plausible-sounding

65

但实际上并没有实现。规则：but was NOT actually achieved. RULES:

  1. 保持所有基准名称、数据集名称和任务名称完全相同。不要更改引用的基准或数据集。Keep ALL benchmark names, dataset names, and task names EXACTLY the same. Do NOT change which benchmark or dataset is referenced.

  2. 仅修改现有的数字分数/阈值，或添加可信的未满足约束。ONLY modify an EXISTING numeric score/threshold, or add a credible unmet constraint.

  3. 如果修改现有的数字分数，请将其提高到足够的水平，以便原始结果明确不满足扰动的要求（例如，如果原始结果为 94.2%，则更改为 95.8%；如果为 51.7%，则更改为 54.5%）。使增加成为一个明显的转变，这样就没有歧义，但在物理上仍然合理。IF modifying an existing numeric score, RAISE it enough so the original result definitively does NOT satisfy the perturbed claim (e.g., if original is 94.2%, change to 95.8%; if 51.7%, change to 54.5%). Make the increase a clear shift so there is no ambiguity, but still physically plausible.

  4. 如果原始声明没有具体数字，则必须添加高度具体、明确的未满足约束（例如，“虽然使用的参数减少了 50%”、“但在零样本任务上完全失败”或“但需要 3 倍的内存”）。使这个约束足够重要，以至于它比原来的约束明显更难满足。IF the original claim has no specific numbers, you MUST add a highly specific, definitive unmet constraint (e.g., ‘while using 50% fewer parameters’, ‘but fails completely on zero-shot tasks’, or ‘but requires 3x the memory’). Make this constraint significant enough that it’s noticeably harder to satisfy than the original.

  5. 令人不安的主张必须是合理的而不是荒谬的。The perturbed claim must be plausible and not absurd.

  6. 保持相同的长度、风格和具体程度。Keep the same length, style, and level of specificity.

返回 JSON：Return JSON with:

  • ‘perturbed_result’：反事实的替代结果主张‘perturbed_result’: The counterfactual alternative result claim

  • ‘changed_detail’：结果的哪一方面被修改‘changed_detail’: Which aspect of the result was modified

创建 MCQ 干扰因素Create MCQ Distractors

  • 主干必须明确但简要地总结问题陈述中的核心挑战（最多 1-2 个子句），然后询问哪种提议的解决方案将实现 { human_date} 所取得的结果。The stem must explicitly but briefly summarize the core challenge from the Problem Statement (in 1-2 clauses max), followed by asking which proposed solution will achieve the Result Achieved by {human_date}.

  • 让它自然地被解读为一个前瞻性的预测问题。Make it read naturally as a forward-looking forecasting question.

  • 不要使用回顾性、过去时的措辞（例如，避免“被引入”或“实现”）。将目标日期视为未来的里程碑。Do NOT use retrospective, past-tense wording (e.g., avoid ‘was introduced’ or ‘achieved’). Treat the target date as a future milestone.

  • 不要在词干中提及正确方法中的任何术语。Do NOT mention any terminology from the Correct Approach in the stem.

J FRQ 响应示例J Example FRQ Responses

GPT-5.4 高分回应GPT-5.4 High-Scoring Response

来源摘要Source Abstract

大型语言模型擅长函数级和文件级代码生成，但从头开始生成完整的存储库仍然是一个基本挑战。此过程需要在提案级和实施级阶段进行连贯且可靠的规划，而自然语言由于其模糊性和冗长性，不适合忠实地表示复杂的软件结构。为了解决这个问题，我们引入了存储库规划图（RPG），这是一种持久表示，通过在一个图中编码功能、文件结构、数据流和功能来统一提案级和实施级规划。 RPG 用明确的蓝图取代了模糊的自然语言，从而实现了长期规划和可扩展的存储库生成。在 RPG 的基础上，我们开发了 ZeroRepo，这是一个图形驱动的框架，用于从头开始生成存储库。它分三个阶段运行：提案级规划和实施级细化以构建图形，然后是图形引导的代码生成和测试验证。为了评估这一设置，我们构建了 RepoCraft，这是包含 1,052 项任务的 6 个现实项目的基准。在 RepoCraft 上，ZeroRepo 生成平均近 36K LOC 的存储库，大约是最强基线（克劳德代码）的 3.9 倍，大约是其他基线的 64 倍。它的功能覆盖率达到 81.5%，比克劳德代码 69.7 分别高出 27.3 和 35.8 个百分点。进一步的分析表明，RPG 可以对复杂的依赖关系进行建模，通过近线性扩展实现更加复杂的规划，并增强 LLM 对存储库的理解，从而加速代理本地化。 [60]Large language models excel at function- and file-level code generation, yet generating complete repositories from scratch remains a fundamental challenge. This process demands coherent and reliable planning across proposal- and implementation-level stages, while natural language, due to its ambiguity and verbosity, is ill-suited for faithfully representing complex software structures. To address this, we introduce the Repository Planning Graph (RPG), a persistent representation that unifies proposal- and implementation-level planning by encoding capabilities, file structures, data flows, and functions in one graph. RPG replaces ambiguous natural language with an explicit blueprint, enabling long-horizon planning and scalable repository generation. Building on RPG, we develop ZeroRepo, a graph-driven framework for repository generation from scratch. It operates in three stages: proposal-level planning and implementation-level refinement to construct the graph, followed by graph-guided code generation with test validation. To evaluate this setting, we construct RepoCraft, a benchmark of six real-world projects with 1,052 tasks. On RepoCraft, ZeroRepo produces repositories averaging nearly 36K LOC, roughly 3.9times the strongest baseline (Claude Code) and about 64times other baselines. It attains 81.5% functional coverage and a 69.7exceeding Claude Code by 27.3 and 35.8 percentage points, respectively. Further analysis shows that RPG models complex dependencies, enables progressively more sophisticated planning through near-linear scaling, and enhances LLM understanding of repositories, thereby accelerating agent localization. [60]

自由回复提示：Free-response prompt:

鉴于由于长期规划和可扩展存储库生成的限制而从头开始生成完整软件存储库的挑战，提出一种可以在 2025 年 9 月之前解决此问题的具体方法。提供：Given the challenge of generating complete software repositories from scratch due to limitations in long-horizon planning and scalable repository generation, propose a concrete method that could solve this problem by September 2025. (A) 高级方法描述，(A) a high-level method description,

（二）技术实施方案。型号答案：(B) a technical implementation plan. Model Answer:

使用存储库优先的分层生成方法，其中模型不会直接端到端地编写完整的代码库，而是迭代地构建和维护包含需求、架构、文件接口、依赖约束、测试和实现义务的显式可执行存储库图，代码生成始终以此图为条件并对其进行验证。其核心思想是将远景软件综合转变为受约束的图完成加上本地代码实现，其中规划器模型提出并修改全局存储库图，编码器模型填充图级合同下的文件，而连续执行、静态分析和测试反馈则更新图以保持数百个文件的一致性。从技术上讲，这可以通过组合图结构存储库内存、基于合同的文件规范、对生成的工件进行检索以及在图修复和代码修复之间交替的验证者引导的搜索循环来实现，从而通过将全局规划分解为持久的、可机器检查的中间结构来实现完整存储库的可扩展生成。Use a repository-first hierarchical generation method in which the model does not directly write the full codebase end-to-end, but instead iteratively constructs and maintains an explicit executable repository graph containing requirements, architecture, file interfaces, dependency constraints, tests, and implementation obligations, with code generation always conditioned on this graph and validated against it. The core idea is to turn long-horizon software synthesis into constrained graph completion plus local code realization, where a planner model proposes and revises the global repository graph and a coder model fills in files under graph-level contracts, while continuous execution, static analysis, and test feedback update the graph to preserve coherence over hundreds of files. Technically, this can be implemented by combining a graph-structured repository memory, contract-based file specifications, retrieval over generated artifacts, and a verifier-guided search loop that alternates between graph repair and code repair, enabling scalable generation of complete repositories by decomposing global planning into persistent, machinecheckable intermediate structure.

来源摘要Source Abstract

最近的呼吸道疾病爆发引起了广泛关注，但大多数呼吸监测仍然仅限于物理信号。呼出气冷凝物 (EBC) 蕴藏着丰富的分子信息，可以揭示对个人健康的不同见解。不幸的是，与样本收集相关的挑战和缺乏现场分析工具阻碍了 EBC 分析的广泛采用。在这里，我们介绍 EBCare，一种基于面罩的设备，用于实时原位监测 EBC 生物标志物。 EBCare 采用串联冷却策略、自动化微流体、高选择性电化学生物传感器和无线读取电路，能够在现实生活中的室内和室外活动中对 EBC 分析物进行连续多模式监测。我们验证了 EBCare 在评估健康参与者、慢性阻塞性肺病或哮喘患者以及 COVID-19 感染后患者的代谢状况和呼吸道炎症方面的可用性。 [61]Recent respiratory outbreaks have garnered substantial attention, yet most respiratory monitoring remains confined to physical signals. Exhaled breath condensate (EBC) harbors rich molecular information that could unveil diverse insights into an individual’s health. Unfortunately, challenges related to sample collection and the lack of on-site analytical tools impede the widespread adoption of EBC analysis. Here, we introduce EBCare, a mask-based device for real-time in situ monitoring of EBC biomarkers. Using a tandem cooling strategy, automated microfuidics, highly selective electrochemical biosensors, and a wireless reading circuit, EBCare enables continuous multimodal monitoring of EBC analytes across real-life indoor and outdoor activities. We validated EBCare’s usability in assessing metabolic conditions and respiratory airway inflammation in healthy participants, patients with chronic obstructive pulmonary disease or asthma, and patients after COVID-19 infection. [61]

克劳德 S4.5 高分回应Claude S4.5 High-Scoring Response

来源摘要Source Abstract

超声心动图是最广泛使用的心脏成像方式，通过捕获超声视频数据来评估心脏结构和功能1。超声心动图中的人工智能 (AI) 有潜力简化手动任务并提高可重复性和精确度2。然而，大多数超声心动图人工智能模型都是单视图、单任务系统，无法从全面检查期间捕获的多个视图中合成互补信息3,4，因此导致性能和应用范围有限。为了解决这个问题，我们引入了 EchoPrime，这是一种多视图、视图通知、基于视频的视觉语言基础模型，经过超过 1200 万个视频报告对的训练。 EchoPrime 使用对比学习为综合超声心动图研究中的所有标准视图训练统一的嵌入模型，并代表罕见和常见疾病及诊断。然后，EchoPrime 利用视图分类和视图通知的解剖注意模块来加权特定于视频的嵌入，从而准确映射超声心动图视图和解剖结构之间的关系。通过检索增强解释，EchoPrime 整合了来自所有领域的信息Echocardiography is the most widely used cardiac imaging modality, capturing ultrasound video data to assess cardiac structure and function1. Artificial intelligence (AI) in echocardiography has the potential to streamline manual tasks and improve reproducibility and precision2. However, most echocardiography AI models are singleview, single-task systems that do not synthesize complementary information from multiple views captured during a full examination3,4, and thus lead to limited performance and scope of applications. To address this problem, we introduce EchoPrime, a multi-view, view-informed, video-based vision-language foundation model trained on over 12 million video-report pairs. EchoPrime uses contrastive learning to train a unified embedding model for all standard views in a comprehensive echocardiogram study with representation of both rare and common diseases and diagnoses. EchoPrime then utilizes view classification and a view-informed anatomical attention module to weight video-specific embeddings that accurately map the relationship between echocardiographic views and anatomical structures. With retrieval-augmented interpretation, EchoPrime integrates information from all

69

超声心动图视频进行综合研究并进行整体临床解释。在来自五个国际独立医疗保健系统的数据集中，EchoPrime 在 23 个不同的心脏形态和功能基准上实现了最先进的性能，超越了特定任务方法和以前的基础模型的性能。经过严格的临床评估，EchoPrime 可以协助医生对综合超声心动图进行自动初步评估。 [62]echocardiogram videos in a comprehensive study and performs holistic clinical interpretation. In datasets from five international independent health-care systems, EchoPrime achieves state-of-the-art performance on 23 diverse benchmarks of cardiac form and function, surpassing the performance of both task-specific approaches and previous foundation models. Following rigorous clinical evaluation, EchoPrime can assist physicians in the automated preliminary assessment of comprehensive echocardiography. [62]

The model employs a multi-task decoder architecture with task-specific heads for segmentation, classification, and quantification, while using cross-view attention mechanisms to synthesize complementary information from apical, parasternal, and subcostal windows into coherent cardiac assessments.

GPT-OSS 高分回应GPT-OSS High-Scoring Response

来源摘要Source Abstract

现有的深度估计方法从根本上仅限于预测离散图像网格上的深度。这种表示将其可扩展性限制为任意输出分辨率并阻碍几何细节恢复。本文介绍了 InfiniDepth，它将深度表示为神经隐式场。通过简单而有效的局部隐式解码器，我们可以查询连续二维坐标的深度，从而实现任意分辨率和细粒度的深度估计。为了更好地评估我们方法的能力，我们从五种不同的游戏中策划了高质量的 4K 合成基准，涵盖具有丰富几何和外观细节的不同场景。大量实验表明，InfiniDepth 在相对和度量深度估计任务的合成基准和现实基准上均实现了最先进的性能，尤其是在精细区域中表现出色。它还有利于在大视点变化下进行新颖的视图合成任务，从而产生具有更少孔洞和伪影的高质量结果。 [63]Existing depth estimation methods are fundamentally limited to predicting depth on discrete image grids. Such representations restrict their scalability to arbitrary output resolutions and hinder the geometric detail recovery. This paper introduces InfiniDepth, which represents depth as neural implicit fields . Through a simple yet effective local implicit decoder, we can query depth at continuous 2D coordinates , enabling arbitrary-resolution and fine-grained depth estimation. To better assess our method’s capabilities, we curate a high-quality 4K synthetic benchmark from five different games, spanning diverse scenes with rich geometric and appearance details. Extensive experiments demonstrate that InfiniDepth achieves state-of-the-art performance on both synthetic and real-world benchmarks across relative and metric depth estimation tasks, particularly excelling in fine-detail regions. It also benefits the task of novel view synthesis under large viewpoint shifts, producing high-quality results with fewer holes and artifacts. [63]
